---
name: contextminning-subagentmaxxing
description: >
  Keep the main-thread context as lean as possible while working. Two moves: (1)
  context minning — after each turn, look for a natural break-off point to condense,
  save state to markdown, and compact - write .md docs to make sure you don't lose 
  valuable context (2) subagent maxxing — before doing work yourself, ask whether 
  a subagent (especially the DeepSeek/Kimi agents via subagent-launcher) should research,
  validate, or execute it so the verbose work never lands in your context. Use on 
  any long, multi-step, or research-heavy task.
---

# Context Minning & Subagent Maxxing

The goal of this skill is singular: **keep the actual work in the main thread's
context as small as possible.** Long contexts get slow, expensive, and forgetful.
Every turn, the most useful tokens should be present and the rest should be
elsewhere — in a file, in a summary, or in a subagent that already finished.

It runs as two continuous moves — a posture, not a one-time cleanup. This file is the
philosophy and the high level; each move has a standalone skill with the mechanics,
syntax, and anti-patterns. **Invoke those for the how.**

---

## Move 1 — Context minning: condense at the seams

When a chunk of work becomes *done and durable* — a plan spec'd out, a bug
root-caused, a long read distilled to one answer — the path to that conclusion is dead
weight. At those seams: **write the conclusion to a durable artifact, then compact from
there.** Prefer landing real work as a megaplan asset (plan file or ticket) over a loose
note, so what you shed becomes trackable work rather than vanishing.

The discipline is *write it down first* — you can only safely forget what you can
re-read. And don't over-do it: compact at seams, never on a timer; a slightly long
context costs less than a lost decision.

→ **`minimize-context`** for the seam catalogue, the `/compact` · `/clear` · `/context`
mechanics, megaplan-asset detail, and the over-minning failure mode.

→ **`context-usage`** to *see* the context filling: a turn-by-turn token-growth chart
read from the session transcript, with every compaction boundary marked. Use it to
check whether you're minning at the right seams or letting context balloon to
auto-compaction. (`/context` is the live snapshot; `context-usage` is the trajectory.)

## Move 2 — Subagent maxxing: do the work elsewhere

**Default to doing work in a subagent, not the thread.** Flip the question from "should
a subagent do this?" to "is there any reason this *can't*?" Its tool calls, file reads,
and reasoning stay in *its* context; only the conclusion returns to you. **Execution is
the headline case** — implementing, investigating, building is where byproduct piles up,
so it's the first thing to hand off, not the last. Be willing to launch **many**, even
in parallel, until you have clarity — that's cheaper than carrying the trail inline.

Two principles carry most of the value: spend the subagent on the *byproduct-heavy*
part and ask it to **return the conclusion, not the trail**; and **use the cheapest
model that can do the job** (cheap scan-work → reasoning → frontier only for real
complexity), preparing the context well enough that a cheaper tier can win. Don't
dispatch if you already have the answer.

**Match the venue to the scale.** A one-shot subagent dispatch is for ad-hoc work —
research, a sense-check, a contained fix. Once something is more than ~a day or two of
work it's a *deliverable*: run it as a **megaplan** (itself launched in a subagent) and
use **`megaplan-decision`** to size the tier/robustness/depth. Past ~2 weeks → an epic.

→ **`subagent-launcher`** for the pathways, the cost-escalation ladder with model
identifiers, and how to shape a self-contained subagent prompt.

---

## The loop, in one line

Every turn: *Did I just hit a seam? → write it down, compact. Am I about to do real
work? → hand it to a subagent.* Keep the main thread holding only what the next
decision needs.

# North Star — Arnold self-healing supervision

**End state:** An agent harness where no worker can be launched onto a spec that
isn't live, a box that won't survive it, or a seed bound to the wrong interpreter —
and where every worker death carries its killer's identity in a typed record that
the recovery loop consumes before it ever retries the same fingerprint.

**Enduring principles**
- One door per invariant: admission, dispatch, and death are each enforced at
  exactly one place; duplicate preflights are deleted, not patched around.
- Deaths speak: SIGKILL, timeout, terminate, restack — every termination emits
  `{killer, signal, elapsed_s}` into the failure ledger. Silent death is a bug.
- Models are admitted, not assumed: a model id must resolve against catalog,
  prefix map, family classifier, and live provider membership at dispatch time,
  typedly rejecting expired or unknown ids.
- Fixes ship on main through the fixer contract; hotfixes that live only as
  deployed-but-uncommitted files do not exist.

**Anti-patterns to avoid**
- Single-scan verdicts treated as sustained truth (wedge kills, restacks).
- Anonymous integer exit codes where a disposition belongs.
- Judgment-based "healthy" claims without positive proof (live pid + advancing seq).
- Redispatch of an identical failure fingerprint without a changed precondition.

**Aligned progress feels like:** fewer incident classes over time, each new stall
arriving with evidence attached and leaving with a root fix on main.

# North Star — Arnold self-healing supervision

**End state:** An agent harness where no worker can be launched onto a spec that
isn't live, a box that won't survive it, or a seed bound to the wrong interpreter —
and where every worker death carries its killer's identity in a typed record that
the recovery loop consumes before it ever retries the same fingerprint.

**Enduring principles**
- One door per invariant: admission, dispatch, and death are each enforced at
  exactly one place; duplicate preflights are deleted, not patched around.
- Deaths speak: SIGKILL, timeout, terminate, restack — every termination emits
  `{killer, signal, elapsed_s}` into the failure ledger. Silent death is a bug.
- Models are admitted, not assumed: a model id must resolve against catalog,
  prefix map, family classifier, and live provider membership at dispatch time,
  typedly rejecting expired or unknown ids.
- Fixes ship on main through the fixer contract; hotfixes that live only as
  deployed-but-uncommitted files do not exist.

**Anti-patterns to avoid**
- Single-scan verdicts treated as sustained truth (wedge kills, restacks).
- Anonymous integer exit codes where a disposition belongs.
- Judgment-based "healthy" claims without positive proof (live pid + advancing seq).
- Redispatch of an identical failure fingerprint without a changed precondition.

**Aligned progress feels like:** fewer incident classes over time, each new stall
arriving with evidence attached and leaving with a root fix on main.

# Agent Goal — make the 24h failure marathon structurally impossible

[North Star](./northstar.md) — this run advances "one door per invariant" and
"deaths speak" by shipping the systemic guard Grok specified in
docs/nbf-grok-verdicts.md (§3), verified by structural tests.

## Objective
Implement, in this repo, the typed worker-disposition control plane that makes the
2026-08-26 NBF failure marathon (11+ failure events, docs/nbf-failure-ledger.md)
impossible to recur:

1. **Unique admission gate.** Extend `require_production_worker_dispatch_runtime`
   (cloud/runtime_attestation.py) into the single production worker-admission door:
   spec translation, catalog row, model-family classification, live omp membership,
   seed/interpreter binding, timeout budget — fail closed before any worker exists.
2. **Wire all three launch doors.** workers/_impl.py raw pair, workers/omp.py
   run_omp_step, cloud/babysitter/launch.py must call the gate; no other
   refresh_/require_ preflight may execute on these paths.
3. **Typed death dispositions.** Every terminate site appends
   `{killer, signal, elapsed_s, disposition_id}` to the incident ledger:
   - launcher TimeoutExpired (subagent-launcher/launch_omp_agent.py)
   - resident/subagent.py worker.terminate()
   - watchdog SIGTERM + wedged-signaling path (wrappers/arnold-watchdog)
   - ensure-megaplan-watchdog restack pkill
4. **Fingerprint redispatch block.** Recovery loop refuses to redispatch an
   identical fingerprint unless a changed precondition is recorded.
5. **Joint model admission test** — one function validates spec↔catalog↔family↔live
   provider simultaneously; a second test asserts expired ids fail typed.
6. **Structural spy test.** Driving `_run_step_with_worker` AND `run_omp_step`
   under a production manifest hits the gate exactly once each; no other preflight.

## In scope / non-goals
- In scope: engine code above + their focused tests; catalog/family wiring already
  on main stays; hot-env pin file gets a comment pointing at the gate (no behavior).
- Non-goals: the 22-milestone NBF chain itself (it runs independently);
  Discord/resident features; CI rework beyond making new tests run.

## Settled decisions
- Model policy (user-pinned): Planner & Oracle & `[XHARD]` = grok-4.6;
  every other role = glm-5.3-flash (`openrouter/z-ai/glm-5.3-flash` via omp).
- Fix delivery = fixer contract: commit in candidate tree, ship to origin/main,
  never hotfix-by-deploy-only.
- Single-scan supervision verdicts are banned; two-scan confirmation pattern is
  the standard for any kill decision.

## Validation
- pytest tests/cloud/test_runtime_attestation.py (existing 42) PLUS new
  disposition/admission/spy suites, all green locally.
- Structural spy asserts exactly-once gating from both doors.
- bash -n on wrapper changes; grep proves no remaining raw refresh_/require_ pair
  on the three doors.

## Done / stop
Done when criteria 1–6 hold with green suites and the structural spy passes after a
fresh `git fetch && git rebase origin/main`. Stop (blocked) only if box evidence
needed for a disposition consumer is unavailable and user cannot grant it.

## Sync policy
Push branch `megado-nbf-guard-0826` to origin when batches pass oracle gates.
Merging to main requires user approval at completion review.

docs/workflow-boundary-contracts/c1/atomicity_failure_table.md:34:| **CP1** | **Before dispatch** — after the scheduler decides to advance to the next boundary but before `set_active_step` writes `state.json`. | `state.json` still shows the previous phase and iteration. No `phase_result.json` for the new phase. No boundary receipt. No new artifacts. `events.ndjson` may contain a `step_start` event (best-effort before dispatch). | `indeterminate` (the `step_start` event may be absent or present; the write to `state.json` never happened so there is no durable record of the dispatch decision). | 1. Read `state.json` current_phase and iteration. 2. Read `events.ndjson` to find the most recent `step_start` event whose phase differs from `state.json`. If found, that event's phase is the intended dispatch target — re-dispatch from that phase. 3. If no `step_start` event exists for the gap, the scheduler's dispatch decision was not committed. Treat as a **no-op**: the previous boundary is still the active boundary; re-attempt the same boundary (idempotent). 4. Emit `reconciliation` event with `ReconciliationOutcome.RECOVERED` and `authority_disposition="re_dispatched"` or `"no_op"`. |
docs/workflow-boundary-contracts/c1/atomicity_failure_table.md:35:| **CP2** | **During result publication — after `phase_result.json` write, before boundary receipt write** — `_emit_phase_result` succeeded (atomic write of `phase_result.json`), but `_emit_boundary_receipt` (which writes `boundary_receipts/{boundary_id}.json` + audit JSONL append) did not execute. | `phase_result.json` exists with the correct phase, invocation_id, exit_kind, and artifacts_written. `boundary_receipts/{boundary_id}.json` is **absent**. Emit `reconciliation` event with `ReconciliationOutcome.RECOVERED`, `recovered_evidence_refs` pointing to the reconstructed receipt and `phase_result.json`, and `authority_disposition="receipt_reconstructed"`. |

docs/megaplan-reference-architecture-20260807.md:147:- simple_fixer/superfixer authority derived ONLY from the exact F01 repair-occurrence tuple (never label, liveness, WBC receipt, or rebuildable projection); no-child-agent gate (fixer is a leaf); singleton exact-occurrence claim; durable two-try unchanged-fingerprint mutation budget; action gates at every mutation boundary.
docs/megaplan-reference-architecture-20260807.md:183:**How it works:** An ascending evidence chain from raw run IDs to proof: (1) every run writes an append-only events.ndjson journal (lifecycle, subprocess, LLM calls with provider request_id + token heartbeats, artifact writes, decisions, cost) — seq-monotonic and gap-detectable. (2) Each phase writes a WBC step receipt (prompt_hash_raw + prompt_hash_canonical, model, provider facts, phase metrics). (3) Phase artifacts capture per-stage output (plan_v*.md + meta, critique flags with stable IDs, gate.json + gate_signals, finalize.json task DAG with one sense_check per task, execution batch evidence, validation_*.json, review.json). (4) Review collects objective review_evidence.json (git base/head SHAs, invocation id, execution audit context, diff). (5) At plan level the CompletionVerdict computes a typed verdict from objective providers (phase_coverage, landed_diff, worker_did_work, green_suite, execution_acceptance_contract, review_disposition, declared_noop) so 'done' is a claim the verdict confirms from evidence. (6) At chain level proof-map.json maps each milestone label to a non-empty list of proof artifact paths; completion-manifest.json binds chain/north-star/brief sha256, milestone labels in order, status done, publication evidence, and proof_artifacts (path+sha256 each, existence-verified). (7) Milestones may declare typed conformance gates (kind: final_conformance_gate); the driver runs the validator subprocess (no shell), requires exit 0 plus a valid milestone_validation_receipt.v1 (current sha256 of validator/conformance/traceability files, git head, timestamps), and blocks milestone readiness/auto-merge/completion/advancement on failure; a post-merge final gate recomputes so the accepted receipt names the authoritative merge commit, then appends the receipt to the proof map and emits the manifest. (8) Dependent chains declare chain_completed launch preconditions with require_manifest:true; the driver re-validates all hashes byte-for-byte — a genuinely completed predecessor is invisible to its successor unless the evidence matches. Gates are evidence-aware: the gate reads accumulated critique flags (id, concern, evidence, category, severity, revise summary) plus iteration-pressure/debt signals; the reviser must account for every relevant finding; success criteria (must/should/info) are validated for verifiability at critique and adjudicated at review (only must failures block). Structural issues surface through megaplan ticket new (out-of-scope observations), the critique ledger (occurrences → reconciliation → dispositions → response/non-action → independent outcome with reopen rules), and the meta-evidence stack (watchdog reports → repair-data → progress-auditor reports → fixer-session summaries/index.md, with the gather step being the ceiling on what the judging model can see).

docs/arnold/native-megaplan-build-forward-plan-2026-08-24.md:205:**Acceptance evidence.** P1 validates and content-addresses the first five revision NO-GO gates: canonical Run Authority disposition, approved zero-blocker ownership, accepted Custody `completion-manifest.json` and proof map, accepted `bounded-incident-projection-handoff.json`, and complete production-vector/canary acceptance. docs/runtime-and-fixer-unification-design-20260807.md:58:3. **One `runtime-manifest.json` per runtime is the ONLY *post-bootstrap* runtime resolver.** It cannot be the literally-only resolver: something outside the manifest must locate the manifest. Define **one stable bootstrap path + one authoritative writer**, and include `runtime_id`, `schema`, `generation`, origin-resolvable SHA/artifact digest, state-root identity, and expected runtime fingerprint. On startup, emit **attestation of the actual module path/digest and mount identity** (not just a declared path). Inventory and disable legacy launchers explicitly (the stale `expected_head`, `Path(__file__).with_name('arnold-repair-loop')`, `.cloud-hot-env` `*_SRC` aliases, systemd units). Schema-versioned, atomic tmp+rename writes under a lock, per-runtime files (not one global file) to avoid write-coherence contention.
docs/runtime-and-fixer-unification-design-20260807.md:136:- **Coordination = fenced, durable job state machine, not a bare lease.** A heartbeat lease alone permits split-brain: a paused holder can resume after a replacement starts; re-enqueueing cannot make partially completed external effects exactly-once (external review: oracle #5, top risk #3). Use a job state machine: `pending → running → committing → redriving → done`, a **dedupe key** (chain UUID + failure fingerprint), a **monotonically increasing fencing epoch** (a holder with an older epoch cannot act), acknowledge-only-after-redrive, isolated per-attempt edits, and quarantine/reconcile of dirty attempts on crash rather than blind re-run. Test pause-beyond-TTL-and-resume, not just process death.
docs/runtime-and-fixer-unification-design-20260807.md:182:- Prove the fix from the executing namespace: run a superfixer tick, assert the **executed tree's content fingerprint (module `__file__` + digest)** == base HEAD — not the declared path.
docs/runtime-and-fixer-unification-design-20260807.md:186:**Exit:** a release generation built from an origin-resolvable SHA is verified in a separate namespace and activated by switching the active-generation pointer, with the previous generation retained for rollback; the resident executes that generation (content fingerprint verified); base source on origin + push-authorized; policy delta is the only copy; base venv exists; data-store writes confirmed.
docs/runtime-and-fixer-unification-design-20260807.md:202:- Port/confirm `repair_lock.py` on the canonical base; extend into the **fenced, durable job state machine** (`pending → running → committing → redriving → done`, dedupe key = chain UUID + failure fingerprint, monotonically increasing fencing epoch, acknowledge-after-redrive, per-attempt isolated edits, quarantine/reconcile on crash). Test pause-beyond-TTL-and-resume.

The branch-transplant-audit.md already documents every quarry area as ported, deferred, or rejected — satisfying the split-plan deletion criteria for `arnold-generalized-pipeline`. However, explicit user approval is required before any destructive git operation, and the local-only commits on `/private/tmp/arnold-target` must be dispositioned. This plan mechanically verifies the current state, updates stale doc references, documents the allowlist, and produces explicit deletion proposals.\n\n**Key constraints:**\n- No destructive git operations without user approval.\n- No new architecture or broad refactors.\n- `feat/arnold-clean-extraction` is the clean base; broad quarry branches are not merge bases.\n- Worktree must stay clean and `python -m pytest tests/arnold -q` must pass.\n\n## Main Phase\n\n### Step 1: Audit branch/worktree/stash disposition against retirement criteria\n**Complexity: 2**\n\n1. **Read** `docs/arnold/branch-transplant-audit.md` and `docs/elegant-arnold-megaplan-split-plan.md` (lines 535–608) to extract the explicit retirement criteria:\n   - Every useful quarry area must have one of: ported-with-verification, deferred-with-named-target, or explicitly-rejected.\n   - Local-only commits on `/private/tmp/arnold-target` must be dispositioned.\n   - Dirty worktrees with unique payload must be preserved before pruning.\n2. **Cross-reference** the branch-transplant-audit tables against the current slice ledger (`docs/arnold/clean-extraction-port-ledger.md`). **Note:** The slice ledger only documents Slices 1–17. The branch-transplant-audit.md section \"Current Build Branch\" (lines 28–58) is the authoritative source for Slices 18–32, which summarizes the full slice inventory. If a quarry area references a slice beyond 17, accept the audit document as verified evidence rather than flagging a missing ledger entry.\n3. **Produce** an explicit disposition table in a new section of `docs/arnold/branch-transplant-audit.md` (titled \"M5 Disposition Verdict\") with these columns: branch/worktree/stash name, disposition (ported/deferred/rejected/preserve), evidence, deletion-safe (yes/no/pending-approval).\n4. **Flag** any gaps where a quarry area lacks a documented disposition. If a gap is found in a branch pre-classified as \"safe to delete\" in Step 4, reclassify that branch to \"pending-approval\" or \"cannot yet be deleted\" as appropriate.\n\n### Step 2: Update compatibility docs and document allowlist\n**Complexity: 1**\n\n1. **Update** `docs/arnold/branch-transplant-audit.md` — add a new \"M5 Cleanup Status\" section noting that Slices 1–32 have landed and M5 is the cleanup sprint. Also bump the \"Last updated\" date in `docs/arnold/m5b-plugin-relocation-map.md` (to reflect document review, not to conflate M5 and M5b milestones).\n2. **Review** `docs/arnold/package-contract.md` — the `megaplan pipelines` references are valid compatibility surfaces (both old and new CLI paths work). ---
name: contextminning-subagentmaxxing
description: >
  Keep the main-thread context as lean as possible while working. Two moves: (1)
  context minning — after each turn, look for a natural break-off point to condense,
  save state to markdown, and compact - write .md docs to make sure you don't lose 
  valuable context (2) subagent maxxing — before doing work yourself, ask whether 
  a subagent (especially the DeepSeek/Kimi agents via subagent-launcher) should research,
  validate, or execute it so the verbose work never lands in your context. Use on 
  any long, multi-step, or research-heavy task.
---

# Context Minning & Subagent Maxxing

The goal of this skill is singular: **keep the actual work in the main thread's
context as small as possible.** Long contexts get slow, expensive, and forgetful.
Every turn, the most useful tokens should be present and the rest should be
elsewhere — in a file, in a summary, or in a subagent that already finished.

It runs as two continuous moves — a posture, not a one-time cleanup. This file is the
philosophy and the high level; each move has a standalone skill with the mechanics,
syntax, and anti-patterns. **Invoke those for the how.**

---

## Move 1 — Context minning: condense at the seams

When a chunk of work becomes *done and durable* — a plan spec'd out, a bug
root-caused, a long read distilled to one answer — the path to that conclusion is dead
weight. At those seams: **write the conclusion to a durable artifact, then compact from
there.** Prefer landing real work as a megaplan asset (plan file or ticket) over a loose
note, so what you shed becomes trackable work rather than vanishing.

The discipline is *write it down first* — you can only safely forget what you can
re-read. And don't over-do it: compact at seams, never on a timer; a slightly long
context costs less than a lost decision.

→ **`minimize-context`** for the seam catalogue, the `/compact` · `/clear` · `/context`
mechanics, megaplan-asset detail, and the over-minning failure mode.

→ **`context-usage`** to *see* the context filling: a turn-by-turn token-growth chart
read from the session transcript, with every compaction boundary marked. Use it to
check whether you're minning at the right seams or letting context balloon to
auto-compaction. (`/context` is the live snapshot; `context-usage` is the trajectory.)

## Move 2 — Subagent maxxing: do the work elsewhere

**Default to doing work in a subagent, not the thread.** Flip the question from "should
a subagent do this?" to "is there any reason this *can't*?" Its tool calls, file reads,
and reasoning stay in *its* context; only the conclusion returns to you. **Execution is
the headline case** — implementing, investigating, building is where byproduct piles up,
so it's the first thing to hand off, not the last. Be willing to launch **many**, even
in parallel, until you have clarity — that's cheaper than carrying the trail inline.

Two principles carry most of the value: spend the subagent on the *byproduct-heavy*
part and ask it to **return the conclusion, not the trail**; and **use the cheapest
model that can do the job** (cheap scan-work → reasoning → frontier only for real
complexity), preparing the context well enough that a cheaper tier can win. Don't
dispatch if you already have the answer.

**Match the venue to the scale.** A one-shot subagent dispatch is for ad-hoc work —
research, a sense-check, a contained fix. Once something is more than ~a day or two of
work it's a *deliverable*: run it as a **megaplan** (itself launched in a subagent) and
use **`megaplan-decision`** to size the tier/robustness/depth. Past ~2 weeks → an epic.

→ **`subagent-launcher`** for the pathways, the cost-escalation ladder with model
identifiers, and how to shape a self-contained subagent prompt.

---

## The loop, in one line

Every turn: *Did I just hit a seam? → write it down, compact. Am I about to do real
work? → hand it to a subagent.* Keep the main thread holding only what the next
decision needs.

# North Star — Arnold self-healing supervision

**End state:** An agent harness where no worker can be launched onto a spec that
isn't live, a box that won't survive it, or a seed bound to the wrong interpreter —
and where every worker death carries its killer's identity in a typed record that
the recovery loop consumes before it ever retries the same fingerprint.

**Enduring principles**
- One door per invariant: admission, dispatch, and death are each enforced at
  exactly one place; duplicate preflights are deleted, not patched around.
- Deaths speak: SIGKILL, timeout, terminate, restack — every termination emits
  `{killer, signal, elapsed_s}` into the failure ledger. Silent death is a bug.
- Models are admitted, not assumed: a model id must resolve against catalog,
  prefix map, family classifier, and live provider membership at dispatch time,
  typedly rejecting expired or unknown ids.
- Fixes ship on main through the fixer contract; hotfixes that live only as
  deployed-but-uncommitted files do not exist.

**Anti-patterns to avoid**
- Single-scan verdicts treated as sustained truth (wedge kills, restacks).
- Anonymous integer exit codes where a disposition belongs.
- Judgment-based "healthy" claims without positive proof (live pid + advancing seq).
- Redispatch of an identical failure fingerprint without a changed precondition.

**Aligned progress feels like:** fewer incident classes over time, each new stall
arriving with evidence attached and leaving with a root fix on main.

isn't live, a box that won't survive it, or a seed bound to the wrong interpreter —
and where every worker death carries its killer's identity in a typed record that

  typedly rejecting expired or unknown ids.
- Fixes ship on main through the fixer contract; hotfixes that live only as

- Single-scan verdicts treated as sustained truth (wedge kills, restacks).

  2051	    write would leave the chain record lagging the live manifest, so the
  2052	    next validate_runtime_launch_seed would raise "chain runtime binding
  2053	    drifted" and re-break the epic on the SAME engine change (grok audit
  2054	    2026-08-18). An engine change must be a non-event everywhere.
  2055	    """
  2056	    from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.chain.spec import load_chain_state, save_chain_state
  2057	
  2058	    state = load_chain_state(chain_spec_path, verify_execution_binding=False)
  2059	    execution = dict(state.metadata.get("execution_binding") or {})
  2060	    runtime = dict(execution.get("runtime_binding") or {})
  2061	    runtime["current_identity"] = dict(bound_identity)
  2062	    events = list(runtime.get("rebind_events") or [])
  2063	    events.append(
  2064	        {
  2065	            "at": now_utc(),
  2066	            "reason": reason,
  2067	            "direction": "manifest_generation_adopt",
  2068	            "to_source_revision": str(bound_identity.get("source_revision") or ""),
  2069	        }
  2070	    )
  2071	    runtime["rebind_events"] = events
  2072	    execution["runtime_binding"] = runtime
  2073	    state.metadata["execution_binding"] = execution
  2074	    save_chain_state(chain_spec_path, state)
  2075	
  2076	
  2077	def _live_manifest_generation(chain_spec_path: Path) -> int | None:
  2078	    """Return the LIVE manifest generation for the chain's session runtime.
  2079	
  2080	    Reads the session runtime manifest (the same path ensure_runtime_launch
  2081	    _seed uses via ARNOLD_RUNTIME_MANIFEST / chain session marker). ``None``
  2082	    when unknown — adopt is then allowed on same-root, matching the
  2083	    ensure-side rule.
  2084	    """
  2085	    manifest_value = str(os.environ.get("ARNOLD_RUNTIME_MANIFEST") or "").strip()
  2086	    if not manifest_value:
  2087	        return None
  2088	    from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.cloud.runtime_manifest import (
  2089	        ManifestError,
  2090	        load_manifest,
  2091	    )
  2092	
  2093	    try:
  2094	        manifest = load_manifest(Path(manifest_value).expanduser().resolve(strict=False))
  2095	        return int(manifest.generation)
  2096	    except (ManifestError, TypeError, ValueError):
  2097	        return None

  2100	def refresh_runtime_launch_seed_for_worker_dispatch() -> Path | None:
  2101	    """Select the accepted generation immediately before a worker dispatch.
  2102	
  2103	    The chain's configured seed remains its orchestration seed.  Each worker
  2104	    dispatch re-reads the accepted manifest under the promotion lock, resolves
  2105	    or creates that generation's immutable seed, and updates the exact seed
  2106	    path inherited by the child worker process.
  2107	    """
  2108	    manifest_value = str(os.environ.get("ARNOLD_RUNTIME_MANIFEST") or "").strip()
  2109	    current_path = configured_seed_path()
  2110	    if current_path is None:
  2111	        return None
  2112	    # Custody boundary: a configured seed must prove cloud-chain authority
  2113	    # BEFORE any early return, including the no-manifest one, so a
  2114	    # standalone-resident seed cannot cross into worker dispatch unchallenged.
  2115	    current = _json_file(current_path, label="runtime launch seed")
  2116	    if current.get("authority") != RUNTIME_LAUNCH_CLOUD_AUTHORITY:
  2117	        raise CliError(RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR, "worker dispatch requires a cloud-chain runtime seed")
  2118	    if not manifest_value:
  2119	        return current_path
  2120	    input_paths = current.get("input_paths")
  2121	    input_paths = input_paths if isinstance(input_paths, Mapping) else {}
  2122	    chain_spec_value = str(input_paths.get("chain_spec") or "").strip()
  2123	    marker_value = str(input_paths.get("marker") or "").strip()
  2124	    if not chain_spec_value or not marker_value:
  2125	        raise CliError(
  2126	            RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  2127	            "orchestration seed lacks chain_spec or marker dispatch inputs",
  2128	        )
  2129	    manifest_path = Path(manifest_value).expanduser().resolve(strict=False)
  2130	    lock_path = Path(f"{manifest_path}.promotion.lock")
  2131	    lock_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  2132	    lock_fd = os.open(str(lock_path), os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o600)
  2133	    try:
  2134	        fcntl.flock(lock_fd, fcntl.LOCK_SH)
  2135	        selected = ensure_runtime_launch_seed(
  2136	            manifest_path=manifest_path,
  2137	            chain_spec_path=Path(chain_spec_value),
  2138	            marker_path=Path(marker_value),
  2139	            chain_runtime_identity=(
  2140	                current.get("chain_runtime_binding", {}).get("runtime_identity")
  2820	    if (
  2821	        raw_seed_path is None
  2822	        or not raw_seed_path.is_absolute()
  2823	        or raw_seed_path.is_symlink()
  2824	    ):
  2825	        raise CliError(RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR, "configured resident seed path is a symlink or missing")
  2826	    pointer = load_standalone_runtime_dispatch_pointer(Path(str(seed.get("project_root") or "")))
  2827	    if Path(str(pointer.get("seed_path") or "")) != raw_seed_path:
  2828	        raise CliError(RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR, "configured resident seed is not the published dispatch seed")
  2829	    if pointer.get("seed_sha256") != seed.get("content_sha256"):
  2830	        raise CliError(RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR, "configured resident seed digest does not match dispatch pointer")
  2831	
  2832	
  2833	def preflight_configured_launch_seed(
  2834	    project_root: Path | None = None,
  2835	) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
  2836	    """Load and validate a CONFIGURED launch seed; require nothing.
  2837	
  2838	    Dry-run deployments keep custody signal without custody obligations: a
  2839	    configured seed is loaded and digest/authority-validated (standalone
  2840	    seeds additionally re-validate live evidence and their dispatch-pointer
  2841	    binding), while an unset configuration behaves exactly as if no
  2842	    attestation existed.  No process status is created, and no network,
  2843	    token, runner, or service surface is touched.
  2844	    """
  2845	    ensure_standalone_launch_seed_binds_root(project_root)
  2846	    seed_path = configured_seed_path()
  2847	    if seed_path is None:
  2848	        return None
  2849	    seed = _json_file(seed_path, label="runtime launch seed")
  2850	    authority = seed.get("authority")
  2851	    if not isinstance(authority, str) or authority not in RUNTIME_LAUNCH_AUTHORITIES:
  2852	        raise CliError(RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR, "runtime launch seed authority is invalid")
  2853	    if authority == RUNTIME_LAUNCH_STANDALONE_AUTHORITY:
  2854	        _validate_standalone_dispatch_binding(seed)
  2855	        validate_standalone_runtime_launch_seed(seed)
  2856	    else:
  2857	        _verify_seed_digest(seed)
  2858	    return seed
  2859	
  2860	
  2861	def require_configured_runtime_launch(
  2862	    component: str,
  2863	    *,
  2864	    target_pid: int | None = None,
  2865	    create: bool = False,
  2866	) -> dict[str, Any]:
  2867	    seed_path = configured_seed_path()
  2868	    if seed_path is None:
  2869	        raise CliError(
  2870	            RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  2871	            "canonical runtime launch seed is required but missing",
  2872	        )
  2873	    seed = _json_file(seed_path, label="runtime launch seed")
  2874	    authority = seed.get("authority")
  2875	    if not isinstance(authority, str) or authority not in RUNTIME_LAUNCH_AUTHORITIES:
  2876	        raise CliError(RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR, "runtime launch seed authority is invalid")
  2877	    if authority == RUNTIME_LAUNCH_STANDALONE_AUTHORITY:
  2878	        _validate_standalone_dispatch_binding(seed)
  2879	    pid = target_pid or os.getpid()
  2880	    attestation_path = configured_process_attestation_path(component, seed=seed)
  2881	    if authority == RUNTIME_LAUNCH_STANDALONE_AUTHORITY:
  2882	        state = standalone_runtime_launch_dir(Path(str(seed.get("project_root") or "")), create=create)
  2883	        _require_standalone_operational_dir(state, "status", create=create)
  2884	        if attestation_path.is_symlink():
  2885	            raise CliError(RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR, "resident process attestation path is a symlink")
  2886	        try:
  2887	            attestation_path.resolve(strict=False).relative_to(state.resolve(strict=True))
  2888	        except ValueError as exc:
  2889	            raise CliError(RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR, "resident process attestation path escaped state directory") from exc
  2890	    if create:
  2891	        attestation = create_runtime_process_attestation(
  2892	            seed,
  2893	            component=component,
  2894	            target_pid=pid,
  2895	        )
  2896	        _atomic_write(attestation_path, attestation)
  2897	    else:
  2898	        if authority == RUNTIME_LAUNCH_STANDALONE_AUTHORITY:
  2899	            try:
  2900	                if stat.S_IMODE(attestation_path.stat().st_mode) != 0o600:
  2901	                    raise CliError(
  2902	                        RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  2903	                        "resident process attestation permissions are unsafe",
  2904	                    )
  2905	            except OSError as exc:
  2906	                raise CliError(
  2907	                    RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  2908	                    "runtime process attestation is unreadable",
  2909	                ) from exc
  2910	        attestation = _json_file(
  2911	            attestation_path,
  2912	            label="runtime process attestation",
  2913	        )
  2914	        validate_runtime_process_attestation(
  2915	            seed,
  2916	            attestation,
  2917	            component=component,
  2918	            target_pid=pid,
  2919	        )
  2920	    return seed
  2921	
  2922	
  2923	def require_production_worker_dispatch_runtime(
  2924	    *,
  2925	    component: str = "worker",
  2926	    create_process_attestation: bool = True,
  2927	    demand_seed: bool = True,
  2928	) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
  2929	    """Admission gate for production phase/backend dispatch.
  2930	
  2931	    Occurrence 12f5e50e0107 (2026-08-26): parallel critique producers and
  2932	    gate reached ``workers/omp.py::_write_phase_output_tool`` via backend
  2933	    adapters that never pass :func:`run_step_with_worker`, then died late
  2934	    with ImportError("omp_rpc host_tools module is unavailable") because the
  2935	    DISPATCHING interpreter was not the manifest-bound dependency-generation
  2936	    interpreter. This helper serves BOTH boundaries with a single contract:
  2937	
  2938	    * Orchestration boundary (``workers/_impl.py``, ``demand_seed=True``):
  2939	      the seed requirement is UNCONDITIONAL — byte-for-byte the previous
  2940	      run_step_with_worker contract (refresh + require + attestation).
  2941	    * Backend boundary (``run_omp_step``, ``demand_seed=False``): enforcement
  2942	      keys off the PRODUCTION binding. With no ``ARNOLD_RUNTIME_MANIFEST``
  2943	      and no configured seed the call is a no-op so manifestless development
  2944	      and unit harnesses keep working; with the binding present it demands
  2945	      the seed and refuses any interpreter that does not realpath-match that
  2946	      binding's dependency-generation interpreter and the seed's own
  2947	      binding. Fails closed typed and early instead of late and confusing.
  2948	    """
  2949	    manifest_raw = str(os.environ.get("ARNOLD_RUNTIME_MANIFEST") or "").strip()
  2950	    if not demand_seed and not manifest_raw and configured_seed_path() is None:
  2951	        return None
  2952	    refresh_runtime_launch_seed_for_worker_dispatch()
  2953	    seed = require_configured_runtime_launch(
  2954	        component,
  2955	        create=create_process_attestation,
  2956	    )
  2957	    if not manifest_raw:
  2958	        return seed
  2959	    actual = os.path.realpath(sys.executable)
  2960	    try:
  2961	        from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.cloud.runtime_manifest import (
  2962	            ManifestError,
  2963	            load_manifest,
  2964	        )
  2965	
  2966	        try:
  2967	            manifest = load_manifest(Path(manifest_raw))
  2968	        except ManifestError as exc:
  2969	            raise CliError(
  2970	                RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  2971	                f"runtime manifest {manifest_raw} is invalid: {exc}",
  2972	            ) from exc
  2973	    except OSError as exc:
  2974	        raise CliError(
  2975	            RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  2976	            f"runtime manifest {manifest_raw} is unreadable: {exc}",
  2977	        ) from exc
  2978	    dep = manifest.epic.get("dependency_generation")
  2979	    dep = dep if isinstance(dep, Mapping) else {}
  2980	    expected = os.path.realpath(str(dep.get("interpreter_path") or ""))
  2981	    if not expected or expected != actual:
  2982	        raise CliError(
  2983	            RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  2984	            "worker dispatch requires the manifest dependency-generation "
  2985	            f"interpreter: expected {expected or '<unset>'}, running "
  2986	            f"{actual} (relaunch through arnold-chain)",
  2987	        )
  2988	    seed_dep = seed.get("dependency_generation")
  2989	    seed_dep = seed_dep if isinstance(seed_dep, Mapping) else {}
  2990	    seed_dep_interpreter = str(seed_dep.get("interpreter_path") or "")
  2991	    if (
  2992	        not seed_dep_interpreter
  2993	        or os.path.realpath(seed_dep_interpreter) != expected
  2994	    ):
  2995	        raise CliError(
  7500	        "auth_channel": worker.auth_channel,
  7501	    }
  7502	    worker.auth_metadata = metadata
  7503	    return worker, agent, mode, refreshed
  7504	
  7505	
  7506	def _run_step_with_worker_legacy(
  7507	    step: str,
  7508	    state: PlanState,
  7509	    plan_dir: Path,
  7510	    args: argparse.Namespace,
  7511	    *,
  7512	    root: Path,
  7513	    resolved: tuple[str, str, bool, str | None] | AgentMode | None = None,
  7514	    prompt_override: str | None = None,
  7515	    prompt_kwargs: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  7516	    read_only: bool = False,
  7517	    output_path: Path | None = None,
  7518	    worker_options: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  7519	    record_routing: bool = True,
  7520	    ledger_phase: str | None = None,
  7521	    ledger_step_label: str | None = None,
  7522	    ledger_selected_spec: str | None = None,
  7523	    ledger_tier: int | None = None,
  7524	    ledger_complexity: int | None = None,
  7525	    ledger_tier_routing_active: bool = False,
  7526	    ledger_configured_specs: tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | str | None = None,
  7527	    ledger_attempt_index: int | None = None,
  7528	    ledger_attempted_specs: tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | str | None = None,
  7529	    ledger_failed_attempt_reasons: tuple[str, ...] | list[str] | None = None,
  7530	    ledger_fallback_trigger: str | None = None,
  7531	) -> tuple[WorkerResult, str, str, bool]:
  7532	    am = resolved or resolve_agent_mode(step, args)
  7533	    agent = am.agent if isinstance(am, AgentMode) else am[0]
  7534	    mode = am.mode if isinstance(am, AgentMode) else am[1]
  7535	    refreshed = am.refreshed if isinstance(am, AgentMode) else am[2]
  7536	    model = am.model if isinstance(am, AgentMode) else am[3]
  7537	    effort = am.effort if isinstance(am, AgentMode) else None
  7538	    resolved_model = am.resolved_model if isinstance(am, AgentMode) else am[3]
  7539	    # Backstop: legacy callers (tests, older sites) still pass a 4-tuple
  7540	    # ``resolved=`` which drops ``resolved_model``. If we ended up with a
  7541	    # codex/claude agent but no resolved_model, auto-apply the pinned default
  7542	    # here so downstream dispatch is never invoked with model=None. The
  7543	    # diagnostic in /tmp/codex_wedge_diagnostic.md shows that this was the
  7544	    # silent path leading to the wedge.
  7545	    if resolved_model is None and agent in ("claude", "codex"):
  7546	        resolved_model = resolved_default_model_for_agent(agent)
  7547	    # Cross-call persistence is only valid for execute-shaped phases. Every
  7548	    # other phase receives all needed context in its prompt, so resuming prior
  7549	    # planner/critic/reviewer sessions risks cache-replay no-ops.
  7550	    effective_refreshed = refreshed or step not in _CROSS_CALL_PERSISTENT_STEPS
  7551	    explicit_agent = _agent_requested_explicitly(step, args)
  7552	    attempted_agents: set[str] = set()
  7553	    if ledger_configured_specs is not None:
  7554	        ledger_fields = fallback_observability_fields(
  7555	            ledger_configured_specs,
  7556	            attempt_index=int(ledger_attempt_index or 0),
  7557	            attempted_specs=ledger_attempted_specs,
  7558	            failed_attempt_reasons=ledger_failed_attempt_reasons,
  7559	            fallback_trigger=ledger_fallback_trigger,
  7560	        )
  7561	        fallback_metadata = {
  7562	            "configured_specs": tuple(ledger_fields["configured_specs"]),
  7563	            "attempt_index": ledger_fields["selected_spec_index"],
  7564	            "attempted_specs": tuple(ledger_fields["attempted_specs"]),
  7565	            "failed_attempt_reasons": tuple(ledger_fields["failed_attempt_reasons"]),
  7566	            "fallback_trigger": ledger_fields["fallback_trigger"],
  7567	        }
  7568	    else:
  7569	        fallback_metadata = _initial_fallback_metadata(
  7570	            step,
  7571	            args,
  7572	            agent=agent,
  7573	            model=model,
  7574	            effort=effort,
  7575	        )
  7576	    # ── Worker launch preflight: produce a content-addressed equality proof ──
  7577	    # before any agent dispatch loop.  Records the actual runtime values and, when
  7578	    # a chain execution binding is available, verifies them against the bound
  7579	    # identity.  A mismatch raises CliError and blocks the worker.
  7580	    from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.cloud.runtime_attestation import (
  7581	        refresh_runtime_launch_seed_for_worker_dispatch as _refresh_runtime_launch_seed,
  7582	        require_configured_runtime_launch as _require_runtime_launch,
  7583	        runtime_vector_sha256 as _runtime_vector_sha256,
  7584	    )
  7585	    from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.cloud.runtime_provenance import runtime_provenance as _rp
  7586	
  7587	    # Select the currently accepted immutable generation at the actual worker
  7588	    # dispatch boundary.  The long-lived chain parent keeps its orchestration
  7589	    # seed; publication only changes workers started after this point.
  7590	    _refresh_runtime_launch_seed()
  7591	    _launch_seed = _require_runtime_launch("worker", create=True)
  7592	    _runtime = _rp()
  7593	    _source_ref = str(_runtime.get("source_revision") or "")
  7594	    _configured_spec = format_selected_spec(agent, model, effort) or agent
  7595	    # Attempt to locate a chain spec for expected-value comparison.
  7596	    _expected: dict[str, Any] = {}
  7597	    from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.chain.execution_binding import (
  7598	        expected_worker_launch_values,
  7599	        find_bound_chain_spec,
  7600	        require_bound_chain_spec,
  7601	    )
  7602	
  7603	    _launch_seed_present = _launch_seed is not None
  7604	    _bound_chain_spec = (
  7605	        require_bound_chain_spec(root, plan_name=plan_dir.name)
  7606	        if _launch_seed_present
  7607	        else find_bound_chain_spec(root, plan_name=plan_dir.name)
  7608	    )
  7609	    if _bound_chain_spec is not None:
  7610	        _expected = expected_worker_launch_values(
  7611	            spec_path=_bound_chain_spec,
  7612	            root=root,
  7613	            runtime_vector_available=_launch_seed_present,
  7614	        )
  7615	    _strict_runtime_binding = bool(_expected.pop("require_full_vector", False))
  7616	    _runtime_vector = ""
  7617	    if _launch_seed is not None:
  7618	        _runtime_vector = _runtime_vector_sha256(_launch_seed)
  7619	        _seed_chain_binding = _launch_seed.get("chain_runtime_binding")
  7620	        _seed_chain_binding = (
  7621	            _seed_chain_binding if isinstance(_seed_chain_binding, dict) else {}
  7622	        )
  7623	        _expected.update(
  7624	            {
  7625	                "expected_root": str(_launch_seed.get("expected_root") or ""),
  7626	                "expected_runtime_vector_sha256": _runtime_vector,
  7627	                "expected_chain_spec": str(
  7628	                    _seed_chain_binding.get("spec_path") or ""
  7629	                ),
  7630	            }
  7631	        )
  7632	    from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.chain.source_admission import (
  7633	        worker_launch_preflight as _wlp,
  7634	    )
  7635	
  7636	    _wlp(
  7637	        source_ref=_source_ref,
  7638	        installed_package_path=str(_runtime.get("import_root") or ""),
  7639	        runtime_revision=str(_runtime.get("source_revision") or ""),
  7640	        runtime_root=str(_runtime.get("import_root") or ""),
  7641	        runtime_vector_sha256=_runtime_vector,
  7642	        canonical_chain_spec=(
  7643	            str(_bound_chain_spec.resolve(strict=False))
  7644	            if _bound_chain_spec is not None
  7645	            else ""

  2923	def require_production_worker_dispatch_runtime(
  2924	    *,
  2925	    component: str = "worker",
  2926	    create_process_attestation: bool = True,
  2927	    demand_seed: bool = True,
  2928	) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
  2929	    """Admission gate for production phase/backend dispatch.
  2930	
  2931	    Occurrence 12f5e50e0107 (2026-08-26): parallel critique producers and
  2932	    gate reached ``workers/omp.py::_write_phase_output_tool`` via backend
  2933	    adapters that never pass :func:`run_step_with_worker`, then died late
  2934	    with ImportError("omp_rpc host_tools module is unavailable") because the
  2935	    DISPATCHING interpreter was not the manifest-bound dependency-generation
  2936	    interpreter. This helper serves BOTH boundaries with a single contract:
  2937	
  2938	    * Orchestration boundary (``workers/_impl.py``, ``demand_seed=True``):
  2939	      the seed requirement is UNCONDITIONAL — byte-for-byte the previous
  2940	      run_step_with_worker contract (refresh + require + attestation).
  2941	    * Backend boundary (``run_omp_step``, ``demand_seed=False``): enforcement
  2942	      keys off the PRODUCTION binding. With no ``ARNOLD_RUNTIME_MANIFEST``
  2943	      and no configured seed the call is a no-op so manifestless development
  2944	      and unit harnesses keep working; with the binding present it demands
  2945	      the seed and refuses any interpreter that does not realpath-match that
  2946	      binding's dependency-generation interpreter and the seed's own
  2947	      binding. Fails closed typed and early instead of late and confusing.
  2948	    """
  2949	    manifest_raw = str(os.environ.get("ARNOLD_RUNTIME_MANIFEST") or "").strip()
  2950	    if not demand_seed and not manifest_raw and configured_seed_path() is None:
  2951	        return None
  2952	    refresh_runtime_launch_seed_for_worker_dispatch()
  2953	    seed = require_configured_runtime_launch(
  2954	        component,
  2955	        create=create_process_attestation,
  2956	    )
  2957	    if not manifest_raw:
  2958	        return seed
  2959	    actual = os.path.realpath(sys.executable)
  2960	    try:
  2961	        from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.cloud.runtime_manifest import (
  2962	            ManifestError,
  2963	            load_manifest,
  2964	        )
  2965	
  2966	        try:
  2967	            manifest = load_manifest(Path(manifest_raw))
  2968	        except ManifestError as exc:
  2969	            raise CliError(
  2970	                RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  2971	                f"runtime manifest {manifest_raw} is invalid: {exc}",
  2972	            ) from exc
  2973	    except OSError as exc:
  2974	        raise CliError(
  2975	            RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  2976	            f"runtime manifest {manifest_raw} is unreadable: {exc}",
  2977	        ) from exc
  2978	    dep = manifest.epic.get("dependency_generation")
  2979	    dep = dep if isinstance(dep, Mapping) else {}
  2980	    expected = os.path.realpath(str(dep.get("interpreter_path") or ""))
  2981	    if not expected or expected != actual:
  2982	        raise CliError(
  2983	            RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  2984	            "worker dispatch requires the manifest dependency-generation "
  2985	            f"interpreter: expected {expected or '<unset>'}, running "
  2986	            f"{actual} (relaunch through arnold-chain)",
  2987	        )
  2988	    seed_dep = seed.get("dependency_generation")
  2989	    seed_dep = seed_dep if isinstance(seed_dep, Mapping) else {}
  2990	    seed_dep_interpreter = str(seed_dep.get("interpreter_path") or "")
  2991	    if (
  2992	        not seed_dep_interpreter
  2993	        or os.path.realpath(seed_dep_interpreter) != expected
  2994	    ):
  2995	        raise CliError(
  2996	            RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  2997	            "worker dispatch launch seed is bound to a different "
  2998	            "dependency-generation interpreter",
  2999	        )
  3000	    interp = seed.get("interpreter")
  3001	    interp = interp if isinstance(interp, Mapping) else {}
  3002	    seed_executable = str(interp.get("executable") or "")
  3003	    if not seed_executable or os.path.realpath(seed_executable) != actual:
  3004	        raise CliError(
  3005	            RUNTIME_ATTESTATION_ERROR,
  3006	            "runtime interpreter identity drifted",
  3007	        )
  3008	    return seed

  1095	def _local_strict_prompt_suffix() -> str:
  1096	    return (
  1097	        "\n\nResponse enforcement: produce the complete phase output as "
  1098	        "exactly one JSON object matching the supplied canonical schema. "
  1099	        "Call the write_phase_output tool once at the end with that JSON "
  1100	        "object as its payload argument. Do NOT put the JSON in your text "
  1101	        "response and do NOT use Markdown fences. The output file is the "
  1102	        "only output that matters."
  1103	    )

  1110	def run_omp_step(
  1111	    step: str,
  1112	    state: PlanState,
  1113	    plan_dir: Path,
  1114	    *,
  1115	    root: Path,
  1116	    fresh: bool = True,
  1117	    model: str | None = None,
  1118	    effort: str | None = None,
  1119	    prompt_override: str | None = None,
  1120	    output_path: Path | None = None,
  1121	    worker_options: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  1122	    read_only: bool = False,
  1123	    free_text: bool = False,
  1124	    prompt_kwargs: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
  1125	) -> WorkerResult:
  1126	    """Run a megaplan phase through a fresh stateless omp RPC session.
  1127	
  1128	    Structured output is enforced locally (local-strict): the model writes the
  1129	    phase payload to ``capture_recovery.output_path`` via the
  1130	    ``write_phase_output`` host tool, and ``model_seam.capture_step_output``
  1131	    reads/validates the file.  Retries are bounded and attempt-idempotent;
  1132	    execute never replays after side effects.
  1133	    """
  1134	    if os.getenv(MOCK_ENV_VAR) == "1":
  1135	        _check_mock_safe()
  1136	        return mock_worker_output(
  1137	            step,
  1138	            state,
  1139	            plan_dir,
  1140	            prompt_override=prompt_override,
  1141	            prompt_kwargs=prompt_kwargs,
  1142	        )
  1143	
  1144	    # ── Resolve the omp route from the frozen B1 contract ─────────────
  1145	    raw_model = model
  1146	    provider, model_id = parse_omp_spec(raw_model)
  1147	    catalog_model = validate_omp_catalog_model(provider, model_id)
  1148	    _, model_id = parse_omp_spec(catalog_model)
  1149	    thinking = omp_thinking_level(effort, provider, model_id)
  1150	
  1151	    work_dir = resolve_work_dir(state)
  1152	    schema = _resolve_omp_schema(step, state, root)
  1153	    # Local-strict capture schema: the model may only emit fields the
  1154	    # canonical schema owns.  ``additionalProperties: false`` makes the
  1155	    # exact-JSON pre-validation below reject unknown schema-owned fields.
  1156	    import copy as _copy
  1157	
  1158	    strict_capture_schema = _copy.deepcopy(schema)
  1159	    # Runtime dialect for the omp AUDIT path: convert oneOf to anyOf (at
  1160	    # least one branch) only. Deliberately NOT the full codex strict-mode
  1161	    # conversion: its required-promotion makes absent optional keys (e.g.
  1162	    # `area` for catalog lenses, `why`) fail the audit on valid output -
  1163	    # models legitimately omit branch-specific keys. Field-level semantics
  1164	    # are still validated inside each matched branch.
  1165	    def _runtime_dialect(node):
  1166	        if isinstance(node, dict):
  1167	            node = {k: _runtime_dialect(v) for k, v in node.items()}
  1168	            if "oneOf" in node and "anyOf" not in node:
  1169	                node["anyOf"] = node.pop("oneOf")
  1170	            return node
  1171	        if isinstance(node, list):
  1172	            return [_runtime_dialect(item) for item in node]
  1173	        return node
  1174	    strict_capture_schema = _runtime_dialect(strict_capture_schema)
  1175	    strict_capture_schema["additionalProperties"] = False
  1176	    normalized_options = dict(worker_options or {})
  1177	    explicit_output_path = output_path
  1178	    if explicit_output_path is None and normalized_options.get("output_path"):
  1179	        explicit_output_path = Path(str(normalized_options["output_path"]))
  1180	    output_path = explicit_output_path or (
  1181	        plan_dir / f"{step}_omp_output.json"
  1182	    )
  1183	    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1184	
  1185	    # ── Response contract (local strict — omp cannot enforce the schema) ──
  1186	    response_contract = compile_response_contract(
  1187	        schema,
  1188	        provider="omp",
  1189	        model=catalog_model,
  1190	        phase=step,
  1191	        provider_schema_available=False,
  1192	    )
  1193	    persist_response_enforcement_attestation(plan_dir, response_contract.attestation)
  1194	    response_attestation = response_contract.attestation.to_json()
  1195	
  1196	    rendered_prompt = render_prompt_for_dispatch(
  1197	        "omp",
  1198	        step,
  1199	        state,
  1200	        plan_dir,
  1201	        root=root,
  1202	        worker=OMP_WORKER_CHANNEL,
  1203	        # The model seam's budget classifier rejects provider-prefixed
  1204	        # names (``anthropic/...``, ``fireworks/...``); the bare catalog
  1205	        # model id is provider-neutral and classifies by family.
  1206	        model=model_id,
  1207	        normalized_model=model_id,
  1208	        tier=ModelTier.ENFORCED,
  1209	        schema=schema,
  1210	        prompt_override=prompt_override,
  1211	        **(prompt_kwargs or {}),
  1212	    )
  1213	    prompt = rendered_prompt.prompt
  1214	    seed_text = ""
  1215	    if not free_text:
  1216	        # Seed the output file with a JSON template so the model's job is
  1217	        # concrete (read template -> fill -> write via the tool), and prepend
  1218	        # + append an unmissable output contract.
  1219	        try:
  1220	            if not output_path.exists() or not output_path.read_text(
  1221	                encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
  1222	            ).strip():
  1223	                from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.workers._payload import (
  1224	                    _build_output_template,
  1225	                )
  1226	
  1227	                output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1228	                seed_text = _build_output_template(step, schema)
  1229	                output_path.write_text(seed_text, encoding="utf-8")
  1230	        except Exception:
  1231	            pass
  1232	        contract = (
  1233	            "\n\nOUTPUT CONTRACT (mandatory):\n"
  1234	            f"1. The output file is {output_path}. It currently contains an "
  1235	            "empty JSON template.\n"
  1236	            "2. Read the template file, then call the write_phase_output tool "
  1237	            "ONCE with the COMPLETE final JSON object as its payload argument.\n"
  1238	            "3. Your text response must be a brief confirmation only - never "
  1239	            "the JSON itself.\n"
  1240	            "4. Do not use Markdown fences anywhere.\n"
  1241	        )
  1242	        prompt = contract + "\n\n" + prompt + contract
  1243	    try:
  1244	        check_prompt_size(prompt, phase=step)
  1245	    except CliError:
  1246	        raise
  1247	
  1248	    timeout_seconds = _omp_timeout_for_step(step)
  1249	    toolset = _prompt_phase_tools(step, read_only)
  1250	    prompt_hash = hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
  1251	    started = time.monotonic()
  1252	
  1253	    # Credentials: the RPC child inherits the Arnold process env; validate the
  1254	    # frozen credential presence up front so a missing key fails closed before
  1255	    # launch (mirrors the legacy provider-credential validation).  At least one
  1256	    # alias variable per route must be set (e.g. ANTHROPIC_OAUTH_TOKEN OR
  1257	    # ANTHROPIC_API_KEY; MOONSHOT_API_KEY OR KIMI_API_KEY).
  1258	    alias_names = _OMP_CREDENTIAL_ENV.get(provider, ())
  1259	    if (
  1260	        provider not in _OMP_NATIVE_CREDENTIAL_ROUTES
  1261	        and not any(os.environ.get(name) for name in alias_names)
  1262	    ):
  1263	        # Native routes (openai-codex OAuth, grok CLI-proxy token, tokenless
  1264	        # Kimi Code OAuth) resolve credentials omp-side; env routes need at
  1265	        # least one key.
  1266	        raise CliError(
  1267	            "authentication",
  1268	            f"omp provider {provider!r} requires one of "
  1269	            f"{alias_names} but none is set",
  1270	        )
  1271	
  1272	    attempt_index = 0
  1273	    attempted_specs: list[str] = []
  1274	    failed_attempt_reasons: list[str] = []
  1275	    last_error: CliError | None = None
  1276	    max_attempts = max(1, _OMP_MAX_ATTEMPTS)
  1277	
  1278	    while True:
  1279	        attempt_index += 1
  1280	        attempt_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
  1281	        attempted_specs.append(format_omp_spec(provider, model_id))
  1282	        client = None
  1283	        try:
  1284	            if seed_text:
  1285	                # Reset the structured-output file to the seed template at the
  1286	                # start of EVERY attempt: a stale file written by a previous
  1287	                # attempt that then timed out must not be accepted by the next
  1288	                # attempt without the model actually calling the host tool.
  1289	                try:
  1290	                    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1291	                    output_path.write_text(seed_text, encoding="utf-8")
  1292	                except OSError:
  1293	                    pass
  1294	            if step in _EXECUTE_STEPS:
  1295	                mutation_before = _worktree_mutation_fingerprint(work_dir)
  1296	            else:
  1297	                mutation_before = None
  1298	            client = _build_client(
  1299	                provider=provider,
  1300	                model_id=model_id,
  1301	                cwd=work_dir,
  1302	                thinking=thinking,
  1303	                tools=toolset,
  1304	                custom_tools=[_write_phase_output_tool(output_path)],
  1305	                env=os.environ,
  1306	                timeout=timeout_seconds,
  1307	            )
  1308	            client.start()
  1309	            try:
  1310	                # Bound the bun host's context growth during long streaming
  1311	                # turns (occurrence 1ac805e5eef9: frontier-phase workers
  1312	                # accumulated multi-GB RSS and were cgroup-OOM-killed under
  1313	                # the 8 GiB ceiling).  Auto-compaction summarises old context
  1314	                # instead of buffering unbounded thinking/tool deltas.
  1315	                client.set_auto_compaction(True)
  1316	            except Exception as exc:
  1317	                # The pinned RpcClient supports it; fakes may not.  Never
  1318	                # fail a launch over a best-effort memory lever, but make
  1319	                # a missing lever observable instead of a silent pass.
  1320	                logging.getLogger(__name__).warning(
  1321	                    "set_auto_compaction unavailable (%s); "
  1322	                    "continuing without context compaction",
  1323	                    exc,
  1324	                )
  1325	            try:
  1326	                client.set_model(provider, model_id)
  1327	            except Exception:
  1328	                # Some fakes/providers resolve the model at launch; tolerate
  1329	                # set_model being unsupported only when the client already
  1330	                # reports the requested model.
  1331	                try:
  1332	                    client.get_state()
  1333	                except Exception:
  1334	                    raise
  1335	            if thinking is not None:
  1336	                try:
  1337	                    client.set_thinking_level(thinking)
  1338	                except Exception:
  1339	                    pass
  1340	
  1341	            call_transaction_id = _emit_llm_start(
  1342	                plan_dir,
  1343	                step,
  1344	                catalog_model,
  1345	                prompt_hash,
  1346	                attempt_id,
  1347	            )
  1348	
  1349	            turn = client.prompt_and_wait(
  1350	                prompt,
  1351	                timeout=timeout_seconds,
  1352	            )
  1353	            _raise_provider_error_from_turn(turn, model=catalog_model)
  1354	
  1355	            try:
  1356	                final_text = turn.require_assistant_text()
  1357	            except Exception as exc:
  1358	                raise CliError(
  1359	                    "worker_parse_error",
  1360	                    f"omp worker produced no final text for step '{step}' "
  1361	                    f"(attempt {attempt_index})",
  1362	                    extra={"raw_output": ""},
  1363	                ) from exc
  1364	            if not final_text.strip():
  1365	                raise CliError(
   430	    ctx: dict[str, Any], *, goal_path: Path, identity_key: str

     1	"""Canonical milestone-source admission and deferred reconciliation."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import hashlib
     6	import json
     7	from pathlib import Path
     8	from typing import Any, Mapping
     9	
    10	from arnold_pipelines.megaplan._core import now_utc
    11	from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.planning.source_binding import canonical_source_identity
    12	from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.types import CliError
    13	
    14	
    15	CHAIN_SOURCE_SCHEMA = "arnold.megaplan.chain_source_admission.v1"
    16	CHAIN_SOURCE_ERROR = "canonical_milestone_source_changed"
    17	
    18	WORKER_LAUNCH_PREFLIGHT_SCHEMA = "arnold.megaplan.worker_launch_preflight.v1"
    19	WORKER_LAUNCH_PREFLIGHT_ERROR = "worker_launch_preflight_mismatch"
    20	
    21	
    22	def _milestone_index_and_idea(spec: Any, label: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    23	    for index, milestone in enumerate(spec.milestones):
    24	        if milestone.label == label:
    25	            return index, str(milestone.idea)
    26	    raise CliError("invalid_args", f"Unknown chain milestone: {label}")
    27	
    28	
    29	def _append_event(state: Any, event: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    30	    metadata = dict(getattr(state, "metadata", {}) or {})
    31	    events = metadata.setdefault("canonical_source_reconciliations", [])
    32	    if not isinstance(events, list):
    33	        events = []
    34	        metadata["canonical_source_reconciliations"] = events
    35	    events.append(dict(event))
    36	    del events[:-100]
    37	    state.metadata = metadata
    38	
    39	
    40	def require_milestone_source_update(
    41	    *,
    42	    spec_path: Path,
    43	    state: Any,
    44	    spec: Any,
    45	    milestone_label: str,
    46	    authoritative_source: Path,
    47	    reason: str,
    48	) -> dict[str, Any]:
    49	    index, installed_idea = _milestone_index_and_idea(spec, milestone_label)
    50	    active_index = int(getattr(state, "current_milestone_index", -1))
    51	    if index < active_index or (index == active_index and getattr(state, "current_plan_name", None)):
    52	        raise CliError(
    53	            "materialized_milestone_source_update",
    54	            f"Milestone {milestone_label} is already materialized; use plan override replan.",
    55	        )
    56	    project_root = spec_path.resolve().parents[3]
    57	    expected = canonical_source_identity(
    58	        authoritative_source,
    59	        project_dir=authoritative_source.resolve().parent,
    60	    )
    61	    if not expected.get("exists") or expected.get("errors"):
    62	        raise CliError(
    63	            "canonical_source_unavailable",
    64	            f"Authoritative source is unavailable: {expected.get('errors')}",
    65	        )
    66	    installed_path = Path(installed_idea)
    67	    if not installed_path.is_absolute():
    68	        installed_path = project_root / installed_path
    69	    observed = canonical_source_identity(installed_path, project_dir=project_root)
    70	    requirement = {
    71	        "schema": CHAIN_SOURCE_SCHEMA,
    72	        "milestone": milestone_label,
    73	        "milestone_index": index,
    74	        "required_at": now_utc(),
    75	        "reason": reason,
    76	        "installed_path": str(installed_path.resolve(strict=False)),
    77	        "expected": expected,
    78	        "observed_at_registration": observed,
    79	        "status": (
    80	            "ready_to_reconcile"
    81	            if observed.get("semantic_sha256") == expected.get("semantic_sha256")
    82	            else "pending_source_update"
    83	        ),
    84	    }
    85	    metadata = dict(getattr(state, "metadata", {}) or {})
    86	    requirements = metadata.setdefault("required_canonical_source_updates", {})
    87	    if not isinstance(requirements, dict):
    88	        requirements = {}
    89	        metadata["required_canonical_source_updates"] = requirements
    90	    requirements[milestone_label] = requirement
    91	    state.metadata = metadata
    92	    _append_event(
    93	        state,
    94	        {
    95	            "schema": CHAIN_SOURCE_SCHEMA,
    96	            "checked_at": requirement["required_at"],
    97	            "milestone": milestone_label,
    98	            "operation": "register_required_source_update",
    99	            "outcome": requirement["status"],
   100	            "old_identity": observed,
   101	            "new_identity": expected,
   102	            "reason": reason,
   103	        },
   104	    )
   105	    return requirement
   106	
   107	
   108	def _bundle_sha(identity: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
   109	    core = {
   110	        "chain_spec_sha256": identity.get("chain_spec_sha256"),
   111	        "milestone_sequence": identity.get("milestone_sequence"),
   112	        "assets": identity.get("assets"),
   113	        "intended_initiative_revision": identity.get("intended_initiative_revision"),
   114	        "initiative_path": identity.get("initiative_path"),
   115	    }
   116	    return hashlib.sha256(
   117	        json.dumps(core, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
   118	    ).hexdigest()
   119	
   120	
   121	def admit_milestone_source(
   122	    *,
   123	    root: Path,
   124	    spec_path: Path,
   125	    spec: Any,
   126	    state: Any,
   127	    milestone: Any,
   128	    milestone_index: int,
   129	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   130	    """Reconcile an unmaterialized source or block before plan initialization."""
   131	
   132	    installed_path = Path(str(milestone.idea))
   133	    if not installed_path.is_absolute():
   134	        installed_path = root / installed_path
   135	    observed = canonical_source_identity(installed_path, project_dir=root)
   136	    metadata = dict(getattr(state, "metadata", {}) or {})
   137	    requirements = metadata.get("required_canonical_source_updates")
   138	    requirement = requirements.get(milestone.label) if isinstance(requirements, dict) else None
   139	    binding = metadata.get("execution_binding")
   140	    launched = binding.get("launched_identity") if isinstance(binding, dict) else None
   141	    old_asset: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
   142	    if isinstance(launched, Mapping):
   143	        assets = launched.get("assets")
   144	        if isinstance(assets, list):
   145	            old_asset = next(
   146	                (
   147	                    item
   148	                    for item in assets
   149	                    if isinstance(item, Mapping)
   150	                    and item.get("kind") == f"milestone_brief:{milestone_index}"
   151	                ),
   152	                None,
   153	            )
   154	    expected_sha = ""
   155	    expected_identity: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
   156	    if isinstance(requirement, Mapping):
   157	        expected_identity = requirement.get("expected") if isinstance(requirement.get("expected"), Mapping) else None
   158	        expected_sha = str((expected_identity or {}).get("semantic_sha256") or "")
   159	    if not expected_sha and isinstance(old_asset, Mapping):
   160	        expected_sha = str(old_asset.get("semantic_sha256") or "")
   161	        if not expected_sha and old_asset.get("resolved_path"):
   162	            old_path = Path(str(old_asset["resolved_path"]))
   163	            if old_path.is_file():
   164	                expected_sha = canonical_source_identity(old_path, project_dir=root).get("semantic_sha256", "")
   165	    current_sha = str(observed.get("semantic_sha256") or "")
   166	    checked_at = now_utc()
   167	    if not observed.get("exists") or observed.get("errors") or (expected_sha and current_sha != expected_sha):
   168	        event = {
   169	            "schema": CHAIN_SOURCE_SCHEMA,
   170	            "checked_at": checked_at,
   171	            "milestone": milestone.label,
   172	            "milestone_index": milestone_index,
   173	            "operation": "milestone_admission",
   174	            "outcome": "blocked",
   175	            "old_identity": dict(old_asset or {}),
   176	            "required_identity": dict(expected_identity or {}),
   177	            "current_identity": observed,
   178	            "reason": "current canonical source does not satisfy the bound/required identity",
   179	        }
   180	        _append_event(state, event)
   181	        from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.chain.spec import save_chain_state
   182	
   183	        save_chain_state(spec_path, state)
   184	        raise CliError(
   185	            CHAIN_SOURCE_ERROR,
   186	            f"Milestone {milestone.label} admission refused before materialization: "
   187	            f"required={expected_sha or 'bound file identity'} current={current_sha or 'missing'}.",
   188	            extra={"canonical_source_admission": event},
   189	        )
   190	
   191	    outcome = "unchanged"
   192	    if isinstance(requirement, dict) or (
   193	        isinstance(old_asset, Mapping) and old_asset.get("sha256") != observed.get("file_sha256")
   194	    ):
   195	        outcome = "reconciled"
   196	        # Adoption is safe here because no plan exists for this milestone and
   197	        # an explicit semantic identity (when required) matched exactly.
   198	        if isinstance(launched, dict):
   199	            from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.chain.execution_binding import active_execution_identity
   200	
   201	            active = active_execution_identity(spec_path)
   202	            launched.update(active)
   203	            launched["bundle_sha256"] = _bundle_sha(launched)
   204	        if isinstance(requirement, dict):
   205	            requirement["status"] = "reconciled"
   206	            requirement["reconciled_at"] = checked_at
   207	            requirement["reconciled_identity"] = observed
   208	    event = {
   209	        "schema": CHAIN_SOURCE_SCHEMA,
   210	        "checked_at": checked_at,
   211	        "milestone": milestone.label,
   212	        "milestone_index": milestone_index,
   213	        "operation": "milestone_admission",
   214	        "outcome": outcome,
   215	        "old_identity": dict(old_asset or {}),
   216	        "required_identity": dict(expected_identity or {}),
   217	        "current_identity": observed,
   218	        "reason": "source identity verified before plan initialization",
   219	    }
   220	    _append_event(state, event)
   221	    from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.chain.spec import save_chain_state
   222	
   223	    save_chain_state(spec_path, state)
   224	    return event
   225	
   226	
   227	def _worker_launch_proof_payload(
   228	    *,
   229	    source_ref: str,
   230	    installed_package_path: str,
   231	    runtime_revision: str,
   232	    runtime_root: str,
   233	    runtime_vector_sha256: str,
   234	    selected_model: str | None,
   235	    configured_spec: str,
   236	    canonical_chain_spec: str,
   237	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   238	    """Build the canonical payload for content-addressed worker launch proof."""
   239	    return {
   240	        "schema": WORKER_LAUNCH_PREFLIGHT_SCHEMA,
   241	        "source_ref": source_ref,
   242	        "installed_package_path": installed_package_path,
   243	        "runtime_revision": runtime_revision,
   244	        "runtime_root": runtime_root,
   245	        "runtime_vector_sha256": runtime_vector_sha256,
   246	        "selected_model": selected_model or "",
   247	        "configured_spec": configured_spec,
   248	        "canonical_chain_spec": canonical_chain_spec,
   249	    }
   250	
   251	
   252	def _worker_launch_proof_sha256(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   253	    """Content-address the worker launch proof payload."""
   254	    return hashlib.sha256(
   255	        json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
   256	    ).hexdigest()

   260	    *,

   253	    """Content-address the worker launch proof payload."""
   254	    return hashlib.sha256(
   255	        json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
   256	    ).hexdigest()
