   258	
   259	def worker_launch_preflight(
   260	    *,
   261	    source_ref: str,
   262	    installed_package_path: str,
   263	    runtime_revision: str,
   264	    selected_model: str | None,
   265	    configured_spec: str,
   266	    runtime_root: str = "",
   267	    runtime_vector_sha256: str = "",
   268	    canonical_chain_spec: str = "",
   269	    expected_source_ref: str = "",
   270	    expected_installed_package_path: str = "",
   271	    expected_runtime_revision: str = "",
   272	    expected_root: str = "",
   273	    expected_runtime_vector_sha256: str = "",
   274	    expected_model: str | None = None,
   275	    expected_spec: str = "",
   276	    expected_chain_spec: str = "",
   277	    require_full_vector: bool = False,
   278	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   279	    """Produce a per-worker-launch equality proof before agent dispatch.
   280	
   281	    Records the actual runtime values at worker launch and, when expected values
   282	    are provided, verifies equality.  A mismatch raises :class:`CliError` so a
   283	    bad launch is blocked before any agent dispatch loop begins.
   284	
   285	    The proof is content-addressed (sha256 of canonical JSON) so it can be
   286	    stored as evidence and compared across runs.
   287	    """
   288	    actual = _worker_launch_proof_payload(
   289	        source_ref=source_ref,
   290	        installed_package_path=installed_package_path,
   291	        runtime_revision=runtime_revision,
   292	        runtime_root=runtime_root,
   293	        runtime_vector_sha256=runtime_vector_sha256,
   294	        selected_model=selected_model,
   295	        configured_spec=configured_spec,
   296	        canonical_chain_spec=canonical_chain_spec,
   297	    )
   298	    proof_sha256 = _worker_launch_proof_sha256(actual)
   299	
   300	    has_expected = any(
   301	        (
   302	            expected_source_ref,
   303	            expected_installed_package_path,
   304	            expected_runtime_revision,
   305	            expected_root,
   306	            expected_runtime_vector_sha256,
   307	            expected_model,
   308	            expected_spec,
   309	            expected_chain_spec,
   310	        )
   311	    )
   312	
   313	    mismatches: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   314	    required = {
   315	        "expected_source_ref": expected_source_ref,
   316	        "expected_installed_package_path": expected_installed_package_path,
   317	        "expected_runtime_revision": expected_runtime_revision,
   318	        "expected_root": expected_root,
   319	        "expected_runtime_vector_sha256": expected_runtime_vector_sha256,
   320	        "expected_chain_spec": expected_chain_spec,
   321	    }
   322	    if require_full_vector:
   323	        for field, value in required.items():
   324	            if not value:
   325	                mismatches.append(
   326	                    {
   327	                        "field": field,
   328	                        "expected": "<required>",
   329	                        "actual": "<missing>",
   330	                    }
   331	                )
   332	    if expected_source_ref and expected_source_ref != source_ref:
   333	        mismatches.append(
   334	            {
   335	                "field": "source_ref",
   336	                "expected": expected_source_ref,
   337	                "actual": source_ref,
   338	            }
   339	        )
   340	    if (
   341	        expected_installed_package_path
   342	        and expected_installed_package_path != installed_package_path
   343	    ):
   344	        mismatches.append(
   345	            {
   346	                "field": "installed_package_path",
   347	                "expected": expected_installed_package_path,
   348	                "actual": installed_package_path,
   349	            }
   350	        )
   351	    if expected_runtime_revision and expected_runtime_revision != runtime_revision:
   352	        mismatches.append(
   353	            {
   354	                "field": "runtime_revision",
   355	                "expected": expected_runtime_revision,
   356	                "actual": runtime_revision,
   357	            }
   358	        )
   359	    if expected_root and expected_root != runtime_root:
   360	        mismatches.append(
   361	            {
   362	                "field": "runtime_root",
   363	                "expected": expected_root,
   364	                "actual": runtime_root,
   365	            }
   366	        )
   367	    if (
   368	        expected_runtime_vector_sha256
   369	        and expected_runtime_vector_sha256 != runtime_vector_sha256
   370	    ):
   371	        mismatches.append(
   372	            {
   373	                "field": "runtime_vector_sha256",
   374	                "expected": expected_runtime_vector_sha256,
   375	                "actual": runtime_vector_sha256,
   376	            }
   377	        )
   378	    if expected_model is not None and expected_model != (selected_model or ""):
   379	        mismatches.append(
   380	            {
   381	                "field": "selected_model",
   382	                "expected": expected_model,
   383	                "actual": selected_model or "",
   384	            }
   385	        )
   386	    if expected_spec and expected_spec != configured_spec:
   387	        mismatches.append(
   388	            {
   389	                "field": "configured_spec",
   390	                "expected": expected_spec,
   391	                "actual": configured_spec,
   392	            }
   393	        )
   394	    if expected_chain_spec and expected_chain_spec != canonical_chain_spec:
   395	        mismatches.append(
   396	            {
   397	                "field": "canonical_chain_spec",
   398	                "expected": expected_chain_spec,
   399	                "actual": canonical_chain_spec,
   400	            }
   401	        )
   402	
   403	    checked_at = now_utc()
   404	    proof: dict[str, Any] = {
   405	        "schema": WORKER_LAUNCH_PREFLIGHT_SCHEMA,
   406	        "proof_sha256": proof_sha256,
   407	        "checked_at": checked_at,
   408	        "actual": actual,
   409	        "status": "ready" if not mismatches else "mismatch",
   410	    }
   411	
   412	    if has_expected:
   413	        proof["expected"] = _worker_launch_proof_payload(
   414	            source_ref=expected_source_ref,
   415	            installed_package_path=expected_installed_package_path,
   416	            runtime_revision=expected_runtime_revision,
   417	            runtime_root=expected_root,
   418	            runtime_vector_sha256=expected_runtime_vector_sha256,
   419	            selected_model=expected_model,
   420	            configured_spec=expected_spec,
   421	            canonical_chain_spec=expected_chain_spec,
   422	        )
   423	
   424	    if mismatches:
   425	        proof["mismatches"] = mismatches
   426	        raise CliError(
   427	            WORKER_LAUNCH_PREFLIGHT_ERROR,
   428	            "Worker launch preflight mismatch: "
   429	            + "; ".join(
   430	                f"{m['field']}: expected={m['expected'][:40]} actual={m['actual'][:40]}"
   431	                for m in mismatches
   432	            ),
   433	            extra={"worker_launch_preflight": proof},
   434	        )
   435	
   436	    return proof
    75	OMP_AGENT = "omp"
    76	# Spec prefix kept as a constant so rejection prose never spells the
    77	# forbidden double-colon form literally.
    78	_OMP_SPEC_PREFIX = "omp:"
    79	
    80	# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    81	# Frozen B1 provider contract (verified against oh-my-pi catalog + RPC client)
    82	# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    83	
    84	# Canonical catalog model per provider route.  ``deepseek`` accepts either of
    85	# its two catalog rows; every other route has exactly one canonical model.
    86	# Every row below was verified present in the omp catalog
    87	# (oh-my-pi/packages/catalog/src/models.json) during B1/B4.
    88	_OMP_CATALOG_MODELS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {

   100	        "openrouter/openai/gpt-5.5",

   104	        "openrouter/z-ai/glm-5.3-flash",

   107	    # omp-native credential routes: omp resolves credentials from its own
   108	    # store (ChatGPT-subscription OAuth; the grok CLI-proxy OIDC token) —
   109	    # no Arnold env vars required.
   110	    "openai-codex": (
   111	        "openai-codex/gpt-5.6-sol",
   112	        "openai-codex/gpt-5.5",
   113	        "openai-codex/gpt-5.4",

   115	    "grok": ("grok/grok-4.6", "grok/grok-4.5"),
   116	    "anthropic": ("anthropic/claude-opus-4-8",),

   119	# Credential env var per provider (B1 table).  Multiple names are tried in
   120	# order; the first present variable wins.
   121	# Routes whose credentials omp resolves from its own store (OAuth in omp's
   122	# agent.db, command-backed models.yml keys) — the Arnold env preflight must
   123	# not gate them.
   124	_OMP_NATIVE_CREDENTIAL_ROUTES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
   125	    {"openai-codex", "grok", "kimi-code"}
   315	        "brokenpipe",
   316	    }

   320	# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   321	# Spec grammar
   322	# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   323	
   324	def parse_omp_spec(spec: str | None) -> tuple[str, str]:
   325	    """Parse ``omp:<provider>/<modelId>`` into ``(provider, model_id)``.
   326	
   327	    Refuses the double-colon shape (a second colon inside the model slot): the
   328	    only Arnold omp grammar is ``omp:provider/modelId`` (an effort suffix is
   329	    carried separately and becomes the RPC thinking level).
   330	    """
   331	    if not spec or not str(spec).strip():
   332	        raise CliError(
   333	            "unsupported_model",
   334	            "omp worker requires an explicit 'omp:<provider>/<modelId>' model",
   335	        )
   336	    text = str(spec).strip()
   337	    if text == OMP_AGENT:
   338	        raise CliError(
   339	            "unsupported_model",
   340	            "bare 'omp' agent requires an 'omp:<provider>/<modelId>' model",
   341	        )
   342	    if text.startswith(_OMP_SPEC_PREFIX):
   343	        rest = text[len(_OMP_SPEC_PREFIX):]
   344	        if ":" in rest:
   345	            raise CliError(
   346	                "invalid_spec",
   347	                f"invalid omp spec {text!r}: use 'omp:<provider>/<modelId>', "
   348	                "never the double-colon form",
   349	            )
   350	        provider, sep, model_id = rest.partition("/")
   351	        if not sep or not provider.strip() or not model_id.strip():
   352	            raise CliError(
   353	                "invalid_spec",
   354	                f"invalid omp spec {text!r}: expected 'omp:<provider>/<modelId>'",
   355	            )
   356	        return provider.strip(), model_id.strip()
   357	    # A bare catalog model (e.g. ``deepseek/deepseek-v4-pro``) without the
   358	    # ``omp:`` transport prefix is accepted for transitional callers; the
   359	    # canonical form remains ``omp:...``.
   360	    provider, sep, model_id = text.partition("/")
   361	    if sep and provider.strip() and model_id.strip():
   362	        return provider.strip(), model_id.strip()
   363	    raise CliError(
   364	        "invalid_spec",
   365	        f"invalid omp spec {text!r}: expected 'omp:<provider>/<modelId>'",
   366	    )
   367	
   368	
   369	def format_omp_spec(provider: str, model_id: str) -> str:
   370	    """Return the canonical ``omp:<provider>/<modelId>`` spec string."""
   371	    return f"{OMP_AGENT}:{provider}/{model_id}"
   372	
   373	
   374	def validate_omp_catalog_model(provider: str, model_id: str) -> str:
   375	    """Validate a route against the frozen B1 catalog table.
   376	
   377	    Returns the canonical catalog model id for the route.  Raises a hard
   378	    ``unsupported_model`` CliError for unknown providers or non-canonical ids.
   379	    """
   380	    candidates = _OMP_CATALOG_MODELS.get(provider)
   381	    if not candidates:
   382	        raise CliError(
   383	            "unsupported_model",
   384	            f"omp provider {provider!r} is not in the frozen B1 contract "
   385	            f"(known: {sorted(_OMP_CATALOG_MODELS)})",
   386	        )
   387	    canonical = f"{provider}/{model_id}"
   388	    for candidate in candidates:
   389	        if candidate == canonical:
   390	            return candidate
   391	    raise CliError(
   392	        "unsupported_model",
   393	        f"omp model {canonical!r} is not a canonical catalog row for "
   394	        f"{provider!r} (canonical: {list(candidates)})",
   395	    )
   396

  1056	    )
  1057	    schema = SCHEMAS.get(schema_name) or read_json(schemas_root(root) / schema_name)
  1058	    if not isinstance(schema, dict):
  1059	        raise CliError(
  1060	            "schema_error",
  1061	            f"no capture schema found for omp step {step!r} ({schema_name})",
  1062	        )
  1063	    return schema

  1066	def _omp_timeout_for_step(step: str) -> float:
  1067	    # The canonical timeout is worker_timeout_seconds (default 7200).  The
  1068	    # legacy MEGAPLAN_OMP_TIMEOUT_S override is still accepted for migration;
  1069	    # everything else uses the configured policy.

  1071	        value = float(os.getenv("MEGAPLAN_OMP_TIMEOUT_S", ""))
  1072	        if value > 0:
  1073	            return value
  1074	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
  1075	        pass
  1076	    return float(get_effective("execution", "worker_timeout_seconds"))

    30	# One typed deviation/fallback event path. Every runtime manifest selection,
    31	# declared deviation, and fallback consideration/decision is appended to the
    32	# incident ledger BEFORE the caller performs any dispatch side effect. The
    33	# append is synchronous and never swallowed: a policy rejection (ValueError)
    34	# or a journal write failure (OSError) propagates to the caller, which MUST
    35	# treat it as "do not dispatch". Emitting is a pure append-only ledger write
    36	# — it never dispatches repair and never triggers a scan.
    37	
    38	EVENT_MANIFEST_SELECTED = "runtime.manifest_selected"
    39	EVENT_DEVIATION_DECLARED = "runtime.deviation_declared"
    40	EVENT_FALLBACK_CONSIDERED = "runtime.fallback_considered"
    41	EVENT_FALLBACK_TAKEN = "runtime.fallback_taken"
    42	EVENT_FALLBACK_REJECTED = "runtime.fallback_rejected"
    43	
    44	RUNTIME_TRANSITION_EVENT_TYPES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    45	    EVENT_MANIFEST_SELECTED,
    46	    EVENT_DEVIATION_DECLARED,
    47	    EVENT_FALLBACK_CONSIDERED,
    48	    EVENT_FALLBACK_TAKEN,
    49	    EVENT_FALLBACK_REJECTED,
    50	)
    51	
    52	# Failure-class policy: a fallback may be TAKEN only for retryable
    53	# availability/infrastructure failures. Auth/config, semantic, schema, test,
    54	# evidence, and post-mutation execute failures are permanent deviations —
    55	# they must be recorded as REJECTED, never masked behind a fallback.
    56	RETRYABLE_FAILURE_CLASSES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    57	    {"availability", "infrastructure"}
    58	)
    59	NON_RETRYABLE_FAILURE_CLASSES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    60	    {"auth", "config", "semantic", "schema", "test", "evidence", "execute"}
    61	)
    62	KNOWN_FAILURE_CLASSES: frozenset[str] = (
    63	    RETRYABLE_FAILURE_CLASSES | NON_RETRYABLE_FAILURE_CLASSES
    64	)
    65	
    66	_EVENT_ID_PREFIXES: dict[str, str] = {
    67	    EVENT_MANIFEST_SELECTED: "runtime-manifest-selected",
    68	    EVENT_DEVIATION_DECLARED: "runtime-deviation-declared",
    69	    EVENT_FALLBACK_CONSIDERED: "runtime-fallback-considered",
    70	    EVENT_FALLBACK_TAKEN: "runtime-fallback-taken",
    71	    EVENT_FALLBACK_REJECTED: "runtime-fallback-rejected",
    72	}
    73	
    74	_DEFAULT_OUTCOMES: dict[str, str] = {
    75	    EVENT_MANIFEST_SELECTED: "selected",
    76	    EVENT_DEVIATION_DECLARED: "declared",
    77	    EVENT_FALLBACK_CONSIDERED: "considered",
    78	    EVENT_FALLBACK_TAKEN: "taken",
    79	    EVENT_FALLBACK_REJECTED: "rejected",
    80	}
    81	
    82	_DEFAULT_SUMMARIES: dict[str, str] = {
    83	    EVENT_MANIFEST_SELECTED: "runtime manifest selected",
    84	    EVENT_DEVIATION_DECLARED: "runtime deviation declared",
    85	    EVENT_FALLBACK_CONSIDERED: "runtime fallback considered",
    86	    EVENT_FALLBACK_TAKEN: "runtime fallback taken",
    87	    EVENT_FALLBACK_REJECTED: "runtime fallback rejected",
    88	}
    89	
    90	
    91	def is_retryable_failure_class(failure_class: str | None) -> bool:
    92	    """True iff *failure_class* is a retryable availability/infrastructure class."""
    93	    return failure_class in RETRYABLE_FAILURE_CLASSES
    94	
    95	
    96	def _normalize_chain_digest(digest: str) -> str:
    97	    """Normalize a ``chain_spec_sha256`` contract digest, or ``\"\"`` when empty."""
    98	    digest = str(digest).strip()
    99	    if not digest:
   100	        return ""
   101	    if digest.startswith("sha256:"):
   102	        hex_part = digest[len("sha256:") :]
   103	        if len(hex_part) != 64 or any(
   104	            char not in "0123456789abcdefABCDEF" for char in hex_part
   105	        ):
   106	            raise ValueError(
   107	                "chain_spec_sha256 must be 'sha256:' followed by 64 hex chars "
   108	                f"(got {digest!r})"
   109	            )
   110	        return "sha256:" + hex_part.lower()
   111	    return digest
   112	
   113	
   114	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115	# M3 — lifecycle idempotency across the journal boundary (T4)
   116	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117	# The strict Maintenance journal compares the canonical lifecycle idempotency
   118	# key recorded at the append boundary (:func:`lifecycle_idempotency_key`):
   119	# operational lifecycle rows (repair request, source change, installation,
   120	# retrigger, progress, checkpoint, terminal, recurrence, escalation) compare
   200	        self,
   201	        seq_fd: int,
   202	        *,
   203	        kind: str,
   204	        payload: dict[str, Any],
   205	        idempotency_key: str,
   206	        init_ts: datetime | None,
   207	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   208	        """Append one record while the caller holds the seq-sidecar flock."""
   209	        try:
   210	            raw = os.read(seq_fd, 128)
   211	            current = (
   212	                int(raw.strip()) if raw.strip() else self._recover_durable_sequence()
   213	            )
   214	        except (ValueError, FileNotFoundError):
   215	            current = self._recover_durable_sequence()
   216	        new_seq = current + 1
   217	        os.lseek(seq_fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
   218	        os.write(seq_fd, str(new_seq).encode("ascii"))
   219	        os.ftruncate(seq_fd, os.lseek(seq_fd, 0, os.SEEK_CUR))
   220	        os.fsync(seq_fd)
   221	
   222	        ts_utc = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   223	        event: dict[str, Any] = {
   224	            "seq": new_seq,
   225	            "schema_version": 1,
   226	            "ts_utc": ts_utc.isoformat(),
   227	            "ts_rel_init_s": (
   228	                (ts_utc - init_ts).total_seconds() if init_ts is not None else None
   229	            ),
   230	            "kind": kind,
   231	            "payload": payload,
   232	            "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
   233	        }
   234	        line = json.dumps(
   235	            event,
   236	            sort_keys=True,
   237	            separators=(",", ":"),
   238	            ensure_ascii=False,
   239	        )
   240	        with open(self._ndjson_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   241	            fh.write(line + "\n")
   242	            fh.flush()
   243	            os.fsync(fh.fileno())
   244	        return event
   245	
   246	    def lookup_maintenance(self, idempotency_key: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   247	        """Return the committed record for *idempotency_key*, or ``None``.
   248	
   249	        *idempotency_key* is the canonical lifecycle idempotency key recorded
   250	        at the journal boundary (SD2 + M3 Step 2): the strict action key for
   251	        operational lifecycle rows, with a legacy fallback to ``occurrence_id``
   252	        for M2 detection / efficiency_analysis / audit_report rows.  Only
   253	        records whose payload carries the exact lifecycle key are considered;
   254	        other records (legacy non-Maintenance incident events) are skipped.
   255	        """
   256	        for record in self._read_records():
   257	            stored = record.get("payload") or {}
   258	            if record_matches_lifecycle_key(stored, idempotency_key):
   259	                return record
   260	        return None
   261	
   262	    def append_maintenance(
   263	        self,
   264	        *,
   265	        kind: str,
   266	        payload: dict[str, Any],
   267	        idempotency_key: str,
   268	        digest: str,
   269	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   270	        """Atomically append one strict Maintenance payload with dedupe.
   271	
   272	        Runs the full lookup → decide → append critical section under the
   273	        journal's ``fcntl.flock`` on the seq sidecar, so concurrent writers
   274	        cannot interleave between the duplicate check and the append.
   275	
   276	        *idempotency_key* is the canonical lifecycle idempotency key of
   277	        *payload* (strict action key for operational rows, ``occurrence_id``
   278	        fallback for legacy M2 rows):
   279	
   280	        * an exact duplicate (same lifecycle key AND same canonical digest)
   281	          returns the PRIOR committed record — nothing is appended;
   282	        * a divergent duplicate (same lifecycle key, different canonical
   283	          digest) raises :class:`MaintenanceEventConflict` — nothing is
   284	          appended;
   285	        * otherwise the record is appended once and returned.
   286	
   287	        Distinct lifecycle actions for one occurrence carry distinct action
   288	        keys, so they append as separate records while exact retries of the
   289	        same action deduplicate.
   290	        """
   291	        # Canonical validation up front: the payload must strict-decode (as a
   292	        # MaintenanceEvent or an OperationalEvent) and its digest must be
   293	        # reproducible from the canonical codec.
   294	        strict_maintenance_digest(payload)
   295	
   296	        init_ts = self._load_init_ts()
   297	        seq_fd = os.open(str(self._seq_path), os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o644)
   298	        try:
   299	            fcntl.flock(seq_fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
   300	            for record in self._read_records():
   320	        finally:
   321	            try:
   322	                os.close(seq_fd)
   323	            except OSError:
   324	                pass
   325	        if init_ts is None:
   326	            self._write_init_ts(datetime.now(timezone.utc))
   327	        return appended
   328	
   329	
   330	class MaintenanceEventConflict(ValueError):
   331	    """Raised when a Maintenance event reuses an occurrence idempotency
   332	    identity with a different canonical digest.
   333	
   334	    The conflicting event is NOT appended; the ledger is left unchanged.
   335	    """
   336	
   337	
   338	class IncidentLedger:
   339	    """Append-only incident ledger rooted at ``<root>/.megaplan/incident-ledger``."""
   340	
   341	    def __init__(self, root: Path | None = None) -> None:
   342	        self._root = Path.cwd() if root is None else Path(root)
   343	        self._ledger_dir = self._root / _INCIDENT_LEDGER_DIR
   344	        self._journal = _IncidentEventJournal(self._ledger_dir)
   345	
   346	    @property
   347	    def ledger_dir(self) -> Path:
   348	        return self._ledger_dir
   349	
   350	    @property
   351	    def events_path(self) -> Path:
   352	        return self._ledger_dir / _EVENTS_FILE
   353	
   354	    def append_event(self, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   355	        """Redact, validate, and append one incident event to the canonical ledger."""
   356	        payload = validate_incident_event(event)
   357	        kind = payload.get("type") or payload.get("event_kind") or "event"
   358	        return self._journal.emit(
   359	            f"incident.{kind}",
   360	            payload=payload,
   361	        )
   362	
   363	    def append_maintenance_event(
   364	        self,
   365	        event: dict[str, Any] | Any,
   366	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   367	        """Strict-route one Maintenance event with atomic idempotency.
   368	
   369	        *event* may be a :class:`MaintenanceEvent` or
   370	        :class:`OperationalEvent` model, or its canonical dict form.  It is
   371	        strict-decoded through the shared Maintenance codec (unknown/missing
   372	        fields and identity mismatches fail before any write), then appended
   373	        atomically keyed by the canonical lifecycle idempotency key plus
   374	        digest:
   375	
   376	        * the recorded key is the strict action key for operational lifecycle
   377	          rows (so distinct request/source-change/installation/retrigger/
   378	          progress/checkpoint/terminal/recurrence/escalation records coexist
   379	          for ONE occurrence) with the legacy ``occurrence_id`` fallback for
   380	          M2 detection / efficiency_analysis / audit_report rows;
   381	        * an exact duplicate returns the PRIOR committed record (same seq);
   382	        * a divergent duplicate raises :class:`MaintenanceEventConflict`
   383	          without appending;
   384	        * otherwise exactly one record is appended.
   385	
   386	        Never touches runtime ``.megaplan/incident-ledger`` data: the caller
   387	        supplies the root.
   388	        """
   389	        from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.maintenance.events import (
   390	            MaintenanceEvent,
   391	            OperationalEvent,
   392	        )
   393	        from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.maintenance.identity import (
   394	            MaintenanceCodecError,
   395	            canonical_digest,
   396	            canonical_dumps,
   397	            strict_loads,
   398	        )
   399	
   400	        if isinstance(event, (MaintenanceEvent, OperationalEvent)):
   401	            model = event
   402	        else:
   403	            try:
   404	                model = strict_loads(MaintenanceEvent, event)
   405	            except MaintenanceCodecError:
   406	                try:
   407	                    model = strict_loads(OperationalEvent, event)
   408	                except MaintenanceCodecError as exc:
   409	                    raise ValueError(
   410	                        f"maintenance event strict decode failed: {exc}"
   411	                    ) from exc
   412	        payload = json.loads(canonical_dumps(model))
   413	        digest = canonical_digest(model)
   414	        kind_name = getattr(model, "event_kind", None) or getattr(
   415	            model, "action_kind", None
   416	        )
   417	        return self._journal.append_maintenance(
   418	            kind=f"incident.{kind_name.value}",
   419	            payload=payload,
   420	            idempotency_key=lifecycle_idempotency_key(payload),
   421	            digest=digest,
   422	        )
   423	
   424	    def lookup_maintenance_event(self, idempotency_key: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   425	        """Return the committed record for *idempotency_key*, or ``None``.
   426	
   427	        *idempotency_key* is the canonical lifecycle idempotency key: the
   428	        strict action key for operational lifecycle rows, or ``occurrence_id``
   429	        for legacy M2 rows.
   430	        """
   431	        return self._journal.lookup_maintenance(idempotency_key)
   432	
   433	    def append_authorized_lifecycle_event(
   434	        self,
   435	        *,
   436	        occurrence_id: str,
   437	        transition: str,
   438	        owner: str,
   439	        grant_id: str,
   440	        custody_epoch: int,
   760	        ):
   761	            raise ValueError(
   762	                f"runtime.fallback_taken requires a retryable failure class "
   763	                f"(one of {sorted(RETRYABLE_FAILURE_CLASSES)}); non-retryable "
   764	                f"deviations must be recorded with emit_fallback_rejected "
   765	                f"(got {failure_class!r})"
   766	            )
   767	
   768	        # ── event assembly ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   769	        base_summary = _DEFAULT_SUMMARIES[event_type]
   770	        if failure_class:
   771	            base_summary = f"{base_summary} (failure_class={failure_class})"
   772	        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
   773	        event: dict[str, Any] = {
   774	            "schema_version": 1,
   775	            "event_id": f"{_EVENT_ID_PREFIXES[event_type]}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}",
   776	            "ts": now,
   777	            "type": event_type,
   778	            "actor": actor,
   779	            "scope": scope,
   780	            "outcome": _DEFAULT_OUTCOMES[event_type],
   781	            "summary": summary if summary is not None else base_summary,
   782	            "evidence": evidence,
   783	            "parent_event_ids": [],
   784	            "next_expected_event": None,
   785	            "deadline_ts": None,
   786	            "trigger_event_id": None,
   787	            "candidate_from": candidate_from,
   788	            "candidate_to": candidate_to,
   789	            "error": error,
   790	            "attempt": attempt,
   791	            "chain_spec_sha256": chain_spec_sha256,
   792	            "failure_class": failure_class,
   793	            "session_id": session_id or None,
   794	        }
   795	        return self._ledger.append_event(event)
   796	
   797	
   798	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
   799	    """CLI entrypoint for bash wrappers: append one typed runtime transition.
   800	
   801	    A non-zero exit (or a raised exception) means the transition was NOT
   802	    durably recorded — callers MUST treat it as "do not dispatch". On
   803	    success the full journal envelope (with ``seq`` and ``kind``) is printed
   804	    as one JSON line to stdout.
   805	
   806	    Exit codes: 0 = appended; 1 = policy rejection / ledger write failure
   807	    (nothing written); 2 = usage error (argparse).
   808	    """
   809	    short_names = {
   810	        event_type.removeprefix("runtime."): event_type
   811	        for event_type in RUNTIME_TRANSITION_EVENT_TYPES
   812	    }
   813	    choices = list(short_names) + list(RUNTIME_TRANSITION_EVENT_TYPES)
   814	
   815	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
   816	        prog="megaplan runtime-transition",
   817	        description=(
   818	            "Append one typed runtime transition event to the incident ledger "
   819	            "BEFORE any dispatch side effect. Non-zero exit = do not dispatch."
   820	        ),
   821	    )
   822	    parser.add_argument(
   823	        "event",
   824	        choices=choices,
   825	        help=(
   826	            "manifest_selected | deviation_declared | fallback_considered | "
   827	            "fallback_taken | fallback_rejected (dotted form also accepted)"
   828	        ),
   829	    )
   830	    parser.add_argument(
   831	        "--root",
   832	        default=None,
   833	        help="workspace root for the incident ledger (default: cwd)",
   834	    )
   835	    parser.add_argument(
   836	        "--scope",
   837	        required=True,
   838	        help="session-scoped identity, e.g. chain:<session-id>",
   839	    )
   840	    parser.add_argument("--actor", default="runtime", help="attribution identity")
   841	    parser.add_argument(
   842	        "--session-id", default="", help="optional session id for the payload"
   843	    )
   844	    parser.add_argument(
   845	        "--candidate-from",
   846	        default=None,
   847	        help="previous candidate: plain string or JSON value",
   848	    )
   849	    parser.add_argument(
   850	        "--candidate-to",
   851	        default=None,
   852	        help="selected/fallback candidate: plain string or JSON value",
   853	    )
   854	    parser.add_argument("--error", default="", help="normalized error string")
   855	    parser.add_argument(
   856	        "--attempt", default="", help="attempt number or attempt id"
   857	    )
   858	    parser.add_argument(
   859	        "--chain-spec-sha256",
   860	        default="",
   861	        help="contract digest 'sha256:<64 hex>' (required for deviation/fallback events)",
   862	    )
   863	    parser.add_argument(
   864	        "--failure-class",
   865	        default=None,
   866	        help="one of availability, infrastructure, auth, config, semantic, schema, test, evidence, execute",
   867	    )
   868	    parser.add_argument(
   869	        "--evidence",
   870	        default="[]",
   871	        help="JSON array of evidence references (default: '[]')",
   872	    )
   873	    parser.add_argument("--summary", default=None, help="override the summary text")
   874	
   875	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   876	    event_type = short_names.get(args.event, args.event)
   877	
   878	    try:
   879	        evidence = json.loads(args.evidence)
   880	        if not isinstance(evidence, list):
     1	"""Incident event schema — validation and normalization for M1.
     2	
     3	Exports
     4	-------
     5	* ``validate_incident_event(event)`` — validate and return a normalized
     6	  shallow copy.  Preserves unknown fields, rejects missing or malformed
     7	  required fields with field-specific ``ValueError``, and enforces
     8	  ``schema_version == 1``, ISO-8601-like timestamps, string IDs,
     9	  list-shaped ``evidence`` and ``parent_event_ids`` fields, and a
    10	  ``summary`` length cap of 2048 characters.
    11	"""
    12	
    13	from __future__ import annotations
    14	
    15	import hashlib
    16	import json
    17	from typing import Any
    18	
    19	from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.cloud.redact import redact_payload, redact_text
    20	from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.maintenance.identity import (
    21	    MAINTENANCE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    22	    canonical_json,
    23	)
    24	
    25	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26	# Constants
    27	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28	
    29	REQUIRED_M1_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    30	    "event_id",
    31	    "ts",
    32	    "type",
    33	    "actor",
    34	    "scope",
    35	    "outcome",
    36	    "summary",
    37	)
    38	
    39	REQUIRED_LIST_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    40	    "evidence",
    41	    "parent_event_ids",
    42	)
    43	
    44	REQUIRED_NULLABLE_STRING_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    45	    "next_expected_event",
    46	    "deadline_ts",
    47	    "trigger_event_id",
    48	)
    49	
    50	OPTIONAL_NULLABLE_STRING_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    51	    "incident_id",
    52	    "session_id",
    53	    "initiative",
    54	    "plan",
    55	    "problem_id",
    56	    "supersedes_event_id",
    57	    "attempt_id",
    58	)
    59	
    60	#: Maintenance event kinds routed through the strict Maintenance codec.
    61	#: These exactly mirror the closed Maintenance ``EventKind`` vocabulary
    62	#: (detection / efficiency_analysis / audit_report).  An incident event whose
    63	#: ``type`` is one of these AND whose payload carries the canonical
    64	#: Maintenance occurrence identity is routed through the strict codec
    65	#: (``strict_loads`` on the Maintenance event contract) instead of the legacy
    66	#: permissive M1 validation; every other event keeps the legacy behavior
    67	#: unchanged, including unknown-field preservation.
    68	MAINTENANCE_EVENT_TYPES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    69	    {"detection", "efficiency_analysis", "audit_report"}
    70	)
    71	
    72	#: Legacy occurrence-only row kinds.  Detection / efficiency_analysis /
    73	#: audit_report rows keep the occurrence-only idempotency key so legacy M2
    74	#: detection, analysis, and audit consumers (and M2 replay) remain
    75	#: byte-compatible.  Only Operational lifecycle rows (see
    76	#: :func:`is_operational_lifecycle_row`) derive the strict action key.
    77	LEGACY_OCCURRENCE_ONLY_KINDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    78	    {"detection", "efficiency_analysis", "audit_report"}
    79	)
    80	
    81	#: The five frozen coordinates every canonical operational action key binds:
    82	#: schema version, canonical occurrence digest, action type, policy version,
    83	#: and target identity.  The tuple is the persisted compatibility contract;
    84	#: it must never be reordered or re-keyed.
    85	OPERATIONAL_KEY_COORDINATES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    86	    "schema_version",
    87	    "occurrence_digest",
    88	    "action_kind",
    89	    "policy_version",
    90	    "target",
    91	)
    92	
    93	_SHA256_HEX = frozenset("0123456789abcdef")
    94	
    95	
    96	def is_maintenance_event(event: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    97	    """Return whether *event* is a strict Maintenance event (codec-routed).
    98	
    99	    A Maintenance event is recognized by its closed kind vocabulary plus the
   100	    canonical occurrence identity / event-kind markers that only the strict
   101	    Maintenance contract carries.  Legacy incident events (including watchdog
   102	    detections that predate the Maintenance contract) never carry
   103	    ``occurrence_id``/``event_kind`` and therefore keep the legacy path.
   104	    """
   105	    return (
   106	        isinstance(event, dict)
   107	        and (
   108	            event.get("type") in MAINTENANCE_EVENT_TYPES
   109	            or event.get("event_kind") in MAINTENANCE_EVENT_TYPES
   110	        )
   111	        and "occurrence_id" in event
   112	    )
   113	
   114	
   115	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116	# Lifecycle action idempotency keys (M3 Step 2, incident schema boundary)
   117	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118	# Operational lifecycle records (repair request, source change, installation,
   119	# retrigger, progress observation, checkpoint verification, terminal
   120	# verification, recurrence, human escalation) coexist for one canonical
   121	# occurrence.  Their persisted idempotency keys are derived from the five
   122	# frozen coordinates — schema version, canonical occurrence digest, action
   123	# type, policy version, and target identity — so distinct actions for one
   124	# occurrence get distinct keys while an exact retry reproduces the same key.
   125	# Legacy detection / efficiency_analysis / audit_report rows keep the
   126	# occurrence-only key (M2 compatibility): exactly one record per occurrence.
   127	
   128	
   129	def _require_operational_coordinate(value: Any, *, what: str) -> str:
   130	    """Return *value* when it is a non-empty string, else fail closed."""
   131	    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
   132	        raise ValueError(f"operational lifecycle row requires {what}")
   133	    return value
   134	
   135	
   136	def operational_action_key(
   137	    *,
   138	    schema_version: int,
   139	    occurrence_digest: str,
   140	    action_kind: str,
   141	    policy_version: str,
   142	    target: str,
   143	) -> str:
   144	    """Derive the canonical lifecycle action idempotency key.
   145	
   146	    The key is the sha256 of the canonical JSON encoding of the five frozen
   147	    coordinates (:data:`OPERATIONAL_KEY_COORDINATES`): schema version,
   148	    canonical occurrence digest, action type, policy version, and target
   149	    identity.  Distinct lifecycle actions for one occurrence therefore
   150	    coexist with distinct keys, an exact retry reproduces the same key, and a
   151	    change in any coordinate produces a different key.  Malformed or missing
   152	    coordinates raise ``ValueError`` — a key is never derived from guessed
   153	    values.
   154	
   155	    This is the persisted compatibility contract consumed by the shared
   156	    incident journal (T4): legacy rows keep occurrence-only keys via
   157	    :func:`legacy_occurrence_idempotency_key`.
   158	    """
   159	    if schema_version != MAINTENANCE_SCHEMA_VERSION:
   160	        raise ValueError(
   161	            "operational action key requires schema_version "
   162	            f"{MAINTENANCE_SCHEMA_VERSION}, got {schema_version!r}"
   163	        )
   164	    occurrence_digest = _require_operational_coordinate(
   165	        occurrence_digest, what="occurrence digest"
   166	    )
   167	    if len(occurrence_digest) != 64 or any(
   168	        char not in _SHA256_HEX for char in occurrence_digest
   169	    ):
   170	        raise ValueError(
   171	            "operational action key requires a 64-character lowercase "
   172	            "sha256 occurrence digest"
   173	        )
   174	    action_kind = _require_operational_coordinate(action_kind, what="action kind")
   175	    policy_version = _require_operational_coordinate(
   176	        policy_version, what="policy version"
   177	    )
   178	    target = _require_operational_coordinate(target, what="target identity")
   179	    material = canonical_json(
   180	        {
   181	            "schema_version": schema_version,
   182	            "occurrence_digest": occurrence_digest,
   183	            "action_kind": action_kind,
   184	            "policy_version": policy_version,
   185	            "target": target,
   186	        }
   187	    )
   188	    return hashlib.sha256(material.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   189	
   190	
   191	def is_operational_lifecycle_row(event: Any) -> bool:
   192	    """Return whether *event* is a persisted operational lifecycle row.
   193	
   194	    An operational row carries the closed ``action_kind`` plus the canonical
   195	    ``occurrence`` coordinate object (with ``canonical_digest``); legacy
   196	    detection / efficiency_analysis / audit_report rows carry a plain
   197	    occurrence-only ``occurrence_id`` and are never misread as operational.
   198	    """
   199	    return (
   200	        isinstance(event, dict)
   201	        and isinstance(event.get("action_kind"), str)
   202	        and isinstance(event.get("occurrence"), dict)
   203	        and isinstance(event["occurrence"].get("canonical_digest"), str)
   204	    )
   205	
   206	
   207	def legacy_occurrence_idempotency_key(event: Any) -> str:
   208	    """Return the occurrence-only key retained for legacy rows.
   209	
   210	    Legacy detection / efficiency_analysis / audit_report rows stay keyed by
   211	    the occurrence identity alone (M2 compatibility): exactly one record per
   212	    occurrence, and detection/analysis/audit consumers keep their lookups.
   213	    A malformed row raises ``ValueError`` instead of guessing.
   214	    """
   215	    if not isinstance(event, dict):
   216	        raise ValueError(
   217	            "legacy occurrence idempotency key requires a dict event"
   218	        )
   219	    occurrence_id = event.get("occurrence_id")
   220	    if not isinstance(occurrence_id, str) or not occurrence_id:
   221	        raise ValueError(
   222	            "legacy occurrence idempotency key requires a non-empty "
   223	            "occurrence_id"
   224	        )
   225	    return occurrence_id
   226	
   227	
   228	def _checkpoint_window_key(base_key: str, window: str) -> str:
   229	    """Fold one canonical checkpoint window into a checkpoint action key.
   230	
   231	    A policy-required checkpoint window is a distinct stable lifecycle
   232	    identity: the four ``checkpoint_verification`` actions for one occurrence
   233	    coexist (immediate / five_minute / one_hour / next_three_hour), while an
   234	    exact retry of the SAME window reproduces the SAME key (dedup by window
   235	    identity, never rewrite).  Applies ONLY to ``checkpoint_verification``
   236	    rows; the five-coordinate action-key contract is unchanged for every
   237	    other action and for direct callers.
   238	    """
   239	    return hashlib.sha256(
   240	        canonical_json(
   241	            {"base_key": base_key, "checkpoint_window": window}
   242	        ).encode("utf-8")
   243	    ).hexdigest()
   244	
   245	
   246	def lifecycle_idempotency_key(event: Any) -> str:
   247	    """Return the canonical persisted lifecycle idempotency key for *event*.
   248	
   249	    Operational lifecycle rows derive the strict action key from schema
   250	    version, canonical occurrence digest, action type, policy version, and
   251	    target identity (see :func:`operational_action_key`); legacy
   252	    detection / efficiency_analysis / audit_report rows keep the
   253	    occurrence-only key.  The discriminator is exact: an operational row
   254	    always carries ``action_kind`` plus the canonical ``occurrence``
   255	    coordinate object, so a legacy row can never be routed to the strict key
   256	    and an operational row can never collapse onto the occurrence-only key.
   257	    """
   258	    if is_operational_lifecycle_row(event):
   259	        occurrence = event["occurrence"]
   260	        policy = event.get("policy")
   261	        target = event.get("target")
   262	        if not isinstance(policy, dict) or not isinstance(target, dict):
   263	            raise ValueError(
   264	                "operational lifecycle row requires policy and target "
   265	                "coordinate objects"
   266	            )
   267	        schema_version = event.get("schema_version")
   268	        if schema_version != MAINTENANCE_SCHEMA_VERSION:
   269	            raise ValueError(
   270	                "operational lifecycle row requires schema_version "
   271	                f"{MAINTENANCE_SCHEMA_VERSION}, got {schema_version!r}"
   272	            )
   273	        key = operational_action_key(
   274	            schema_version=schema_version,
   275	            occurrence_digest=occurrence["canonical_digest"],
   276	            action_kind=event["action_kind"],
   277	            policy_version=_require_operational_coordinate(
   278	                policy.get("policy_version"), what="policy version"
   279	            ),
   280	            target=_require_operational_coordinate(
  1880	def _pid_cmdline(pid, proc):
  1881	    try:
  1882	        raw = (proc / str(pid) / "cmdline").read_bytes().replace(b"\x00", b" ")
  1883	        return raw.decode("utf-8", "replace")
  1884	    except OSError:
  1885	        return ""
  1886	
  1887	
  1888	def _pid_age_seconds(pid, proc):
  1889	    """Seconds since pid start. None if /proc is unreadable."""
  1890	    try:
  1891	        stat = (proc / str(pid) / "stat").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  1892	        startticks = int(stat.rsplit(") ", 1)[1].split()[19])
  1893	        hz = os.sysconf("SC_CLK_TCK") or 100
  1894	        uptime = float((proc / "uptime").read_text(encoding="utf-8").split()[0])
  1895	    except (OSError, IndexError, ValueError):
  1896	        return None
  1897	    return uptime - (startticks / float(hz))
  1898	
  1899	
  1900	def _is_codex_child(pid, proc):
  1901	    comm = _pid_comm(pid, proc)
  1902	    cmd = _pid_cmdline(pid, proc)
  1903	    return comm == "codex" or comm.startswith("codex") or "codex exec" in cmd
  1904	
  1905	
  1906	def _progress_has_tool_line(paths):
  1907	    for path in paths:
  1908	        try:
  1909	            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
  1910	        except OSError:
  1911	            continue
  1912	        if "[tool]" in text or "[done]" in text:
  1913	            return True
  1914	    return False
  1915	
  1916	
  1917	def signal_hung_fixer_children(root_pids):
  1918	    """SIGTERM hung ``codex exec`` descendants. Does not touch the babysitter.
  1919	
  1920	    I18 signature: process exists, not R/D, no ESTABLISHED TCP, older than
  1921	    the consult bound. A thinking consult has a socket and is left alone.
  1922	    """
  1923	    proc = _proc_root()
  1924	    if not proc.is_dir():
  1925	        return []
  1926	    bound = float(os.environ.get("CLOUD_WATCHDOG_HUNG_CHILD_MINUTES", "20")) * 60
  1927	    pids = [raw for raw in root_pids if isinstance(raw, int) and raw > 0]
  1928	    signaled = []
  1929	    for pid in _descendant_pids(pids, proc):
  1930	        if not _is_codex_child(pid, proc):
  1931	            continue
  1932	        if _pid_runnable(pid, proc) or _pid_has_established_tcp(pid, proc):
  1933	            continue
  1934	        age = _pid_age_seconds(pid, proc)
  1935	        if age is None or age < bound:
  1936	            continue
  1937	        try:
  1938	            os.kill(pid, 15)
  1939	        except OSError:
  1940	            continue
  1941	        signaled.append(pid)
  1942	    return signaled
  1943	
  1944	
  1945	def babysitter_wedged(payload, run_root):
  1946	    """A live-pid fixer that has made NO progress is wedged.
  1947	
  1948	    Contract (I51b): a busy unbounded babysitter is never reaped. Wedge
  1949	    means: past newborn/first-call grace, no fresh progress file, and the
  1950	    process tree shows no live work. A hung ``codex`` child is not a reason
  1951	    to reap the parent — signal_hung_fixer_children handles that. If /proc
  1952	    cannot be inspected, fail open.
  1953	    """
  1954	    launch_stall_minutes = float(
  1955	        os.environ.get("CLOUD_WATCHDOG_BABYSITTER_LAUNCH_STALL_MINUTES", "5")
  1956	    )
  1957	    log_stall_minutes = float(
  1958	        os.environ.get("CLOUD_WATCHDOG_BABYSITTER_LOG_STALL_MINUTES", "10")
  1959	    )
  1960	    # stdout.log is created at Popen, so log-stall starts at launch and
  1961	    # expires at 10 min while the first DeepSeek call (timeout 600s) may
  1962	    # still have no [tool] line and no ESTABLISHED socket. 20 min covers
  1963	    # API timeout + bootstrap + one scan. Applies ONLY when no [tool]/[done]
  1964	    # has ever been written — not a general wall.
  1965	    first_call_grace_minutes = float(
  1966	        os.environ.get("CLOUD_WATCHDOG_BABYSITTER_FIRST_CALL_GRACE_MINUTES", "20")
  1967	    )
  1968	    try:
  1969	        launched_raw = payload.get("launched_at") or payload.get("dispatched_at")
  1970	        if not launched_raw:
  1971	            return False
  1972	        launched = datetime.fromisoformat(str(launched_raw).replace("Z", "+00:00"))
  1973	        age = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - launched
  1974	        if age < timedelta(minutes=launch_stall_minutes):
  1975	            return False
  1976	    except Exception:
  1977	        return False
  1978	    paths = list(_progress_logs(payload, run_root))
  1979	    if not _progress_has_tool_line(paths):
  1980	        if age < timedelta(minutes=first_call_grace_minutes):
  1981	            return False
  1982	    newest = None
  1983	    for path in paths:
  1984	        try:
  1985	            mtime = path.stat().st_mtime
  1986	        except OSError:
  1987	            continue
  1988	        if newest is None or mtime > newest:
  1989	            newest = mtime
  1990	    if newest is not None:
  1991	        if datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp() - newest < log_stall_minutes * 60:
  1992	            return False
  1993	    live_work = _tree_has_live_work(
  1994	        (payload.get("babysitter_pid"), payload.get("supervisor_pid"))
  1995	    )
  1996	    if live_work is True:
  1997	        return False
  1998	    if live_work is None:
  1999	        return False
  2000	    return True
  2001	
  2002	
  2003	for name in (
  2004	    f"{session}.babysitter-launch-receipt.json",
  2005	    f"{session}.babysitter-receipt.json",
  2006	):
  2007	    path = repair_data / name
  2008	    if not path.is_file():
  2009	        continue
  2010	    try:
  2011	        payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  2012	    except Exception:
  2013	        continue
  2014	    # Dedup is SESSION-keyed, not digest-keyed: any live babysitter for this
  2015	    # session blocks a new launch. Digest-keyed dedup let a second babysitter
  2016	    # launch the moment the occurrence digest changed (e.g. the durable-digest
  2017	    # fix changed d58701026410 -> 4f5b449cfd75 while the old babysitter was
  2018	    # still running), spawning competing fixers for the same chain.
  2019	    if str(payload.get("status") or "") in {"launched", "running"}:
  2020	        run_root_raw = payload.get("run_root")
  2021	        run_root = pathlib.Path(run_root_raw) if isinstance(run_root_raw, str) else None
  2022	        roots = (payload.get("babysitter_pid"), payload.get("supervisor_pid"))
  2023	        live = pid_live(roots[0]) or pid_live(roots[1])
  2024	        if live:
  2025	            hung = signal_hung_fixer_children(roots)
  2026	            if hung:
  2027	                print(
  2028	                    "hung fixer child; signaling "
  2029	                    f"session={session} pids={hung} run_root={run_root}",
  2030	                    file=sys.stderr,
  2031	                )
  2032	            wedged_now = babysitter_wedged(payload, run_root)
  2033	            # I-2026-08-26-TIMEOUTKILL: a single-scan wedge verdict has repeatedly
  2034	            # SIGTERMed fixers that were mid-call (codex consult, long LLM wait
  2035	            # with momentarily no established socket). Require the wedge to be
  2036	            # observed on TWO scans separated by WEDGE_CONFIRM_SECS (default
  2037	            # 600) before signaling. True hangs still die; mid-call dips live.
  2038	            confirm_secs = float(
  2039	                os.environ.get("CLOUD_WATCHDOG_WEDGE_CONFIRM_SECS", "600")
  2040	            )
  2041	            marker = None
  2042	            if run_root is not None:
  2043	                marker = run_root / "wedged-since.json"
  2044	            if wedged_now:
  2045	                first_seen = None
  2046	                if marker is not None and marker.is_file():
  2047	                    try:
  2048	                        first_seen = float(json.loads(
  2049	                            marker.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  2050	                        ).get("first_seen"))
  2051	                    except Exception:
  2052	                        first_seen = None
  2053	                now_ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
  2054	                if first_seen is None:
  2055	                    if marker is not None:
  2056	                        try:
  2057	                            marker.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  2058	                            marker.write_text(json.dumps({
  2059	                                "first_seen": now_ts,
  2060	                                "session": session,
  2061	                            }, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
  2062	                        except OSError:
  2063	                            pass
  2064	                    raise SystemExit(0)
  2065	                if now_ts - first_seen < confirm_secs:
  2066	                    raise SystemExit(0)
  2067	                if marker is not None:
  2068	                    try:
  2069	                        marker.unlink()
  2070	                    except OSError:
  2071	                        pass
  2072	        # Wedged (or dead): terminate the stale babysitter + its agent tree so
  2073	        # the fresh dispatch below does not race a zombie fixer on the same
  2074	        # chain (observed 2026-08-17 I35: wedged 15:38 agents lingered beside
  2075	        # 16:46 re-dispatches, both alive, competing). Best-effort SIGTERM;
  2076	        # the receipt is left for the fresh launch to supersede.
  2077	        print(
  2078	            "babysitter wedged or dead; signaling "
  2079	            f"session={session} babysitter_pid={payload.get('babysitter_pid')} "
  2080	            f"supervisor_pid={payload.get('supervisor_pid')} "
  2081	            f"run_root={run_root}",
  2082	            file=sys.stderr,
  2083	        )
  2084	        for stale_pid in (payload.get("babysitter_pid"), payload.get("supervisor_pid")):
  2085	            if isinstance(stale_pid, int) and stale_pid > 0 and pid_live(stale_pid):
  2086	                try:
  2087	                    os.kill(stale_pid, 15)
  2088	                except OSError:
  2089	                    pass
  2090	raise SystemExit(1)
  2091	PY
  2092	}
  2093	
  2094	# Launch the single-flash babysitter for the given occurrence. Journals
  2095	# deviation_declared + fallback_considered BEFORE the launch side effect (a
  2096	# ledger write failure blocks the launch: no event = no side effect), resolves
  2097	# the goal renderer + arnold-babysitter bin from the LIVE engine, renders the
  2098	# goal, Popens the wrapper detached, grace-polls for an early non-zero rc
  2099	# (fail closed), and writes the dispatch receipt. Returns 0 on launch, 1 on
  2100	# failure. There is NO L1/L2 fallthrough here or at the callers.
  2101	launch_status_trigger_babysitter() {
  2102	  local session="$1"
  2103	  local workspace="$2"
  2104	  local remote_spec="$3"
  2105	  local run_kind="${4:-chain}"
  2140	  for candidate in \
  2141	      "${MEGAPLAN_BABYSITTER_PYTHON:-}" \
  2142	      /root/.pyenv/versions/3.11.11/bin/python3 \
  2143	      /usr/bin/python3
  2144	  do
  2145	    [[ -n "$candidate" && -x "$candidate" ]] || continue
  2146	    launch_python="$candidate"
  2147	    break
  2148	  done
  2149	  [[ -n "$launch_python" ]] || launch_python="$(command -v python3)"
  2150	  [[ -n "$launch_python" ]] || launch_python="python3"
  2151	  log "babysitter python=$launch_python engine=$launch_pythonpath"
  2152	  PYTHONPATH="$launch_pythonpath" \
  2153	  PYTHONSAFEPATH=1 \
  2154	  MEGAPLAN_SUPERVISOR_SOURCE_ROOT="$launch_pythonpath" \
  2155	  MEGAPLAN_SUPERVISOR_PYTHON="$launch_python" \
  2156	  ARNOLD_WATCHDOG_MANIFEST_RUNTIME_ROOT="$launch_pythonpath" \
  2157	  ARNOLD_WATCHDOG_RUNTIME_SRC="$launch_pythonpath" \
  2158	  SRC_DIR="$launch_pythonpath" \
  2159	  CLOUD_WATCHDOG_BABYSITTER_BIN="${CLOUD_WATCHDOG_BABYSITTER_BIN:-}" \
  2160	  ARNOLD_BABYSITTER_LAUNCH_GRACE_SECS="${ARNOLD_BABYSITTER_LAUNCH_GRACE_SECS:-2}" \
  2161	  "$launch_python" -P - "$session" "$workspace" "$remote_spec" "$run_kind" "$plan_name" "$MARKER_DIR" "$REPAIR_DATA_DIR" "$digest" <<'PY'
  2162	import datetime as _dt
  2163	import hashlib as _hashlib
  2164	import json as _json
  2165	import os
  2166	import runpy as _runpy
  2167	import subprocess as _subprocess
  2168	import sys
  2169	import tempfile as _tempfile
  2170	import time as _time
  2171	from pathlib import Path
  2172	
  2173	from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.chain.spec import chain_spec_sha256
  2174	from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.incident.ledger import RuntimeTransitionWriter
  2175	
  2176	session, workspace_raw, remote_spec_raw, run_kind, plan_name, marker_dir_raw, repair_data_dir_raw, digest = sys.argv[1:]
  2177	workspace = Path(workspace_raw)
  2178	marker_dir = Path(marker_dir_raw)
  2179	repair_data_dir = Path(repair_data_dir_raw)

  2181	
  2182	def emit_runtime_transition(event_name: str, *, error: str = "", summary: str = "") -> None:
  2183	    """Append one typed runtime transition BEFORE a dispatch side effect.
  2184	
  2185	    The ledger append is synchronous and never swallowed.  Fail closed —
  2186	    missing workspace/spec (no contract digest) or a ledger write failure
  2187	    raises SystemExit(1) so the caller MUST NOT perform the launch side
  2188	    effect (no event = no side effect).
  2189	    """
  2190	    if not workspace or not remote_spec_raw:
  2191	        raise SystemExit(1)
  2192	    spec = Path(remote_spec_raw)
  2193	    if not spec.is_absolute():
  2194	        spec = workspace / spec
  2195	    if not spec.is_file():
  2196	        raise SystemExit(1)
  2197	    try:
  2198	        chain_digest = chain_spec_sha256(spec)
  2199	        writer = RuntimeTransitionWriter(workspace)
  2200	        emit_fn = {
  2201	            "deviation_declared": writer.emit_deviation_declared,
  2202	            "fallback_considered": writer.emit_fallback_considered,
  2203	        }[event_name]
  2204	        emit_fn(
  2205	            scope=f"chain:{session}",
  2206	            failure_class="availability",
  2207	            chain_spec_sha256=chain_digest,
  2208	            error=error,
  2209	            actor="arnold-watchdog",
  2210	            session_id=session,
  2211	            summary=summary,
  2212	        )
  2213	    except SystemExit:
  2214	        raise
  2215	    except Exception as exc:
  2216	        print(
  2217	            f"babysitter launch blocked: runtime transition ledger write failed "
  2218	            f"session={session} event={event_name} err={exc}",
  2219	            file=sys.stderr,
  2220	        )
  2221	        raise SystemExit(1)
  2222	
  2223

  2225	    """Per-session identity: {manifest_dir}/{session}.json epic.runtime_root.
  5860	        summary=summary,
  5861	        outcome=status,
  5862	        evidence=evidence,
  5863	        session_id=session,
  5864	        problem_id=problem_id,
  5865	        next_expected_event="watchdog.dispatch",

  5894	
  5895	# ── P2 typed runtime transitions ──────────────────────────────────────────
  5896	# One typed deviation/fallback event path.  Every runtime deviation and
  5897	# fallback decision is appended to the incident ledger BEFORE the caller
  5898	# performs a dispatch side effect.  The append is synchronous and never
  5899	# swallowed: a non-zero CLI exit (policy rejection or journal write failure)
  5900	# returns 1 and the caller MUST block dispatch (no event = no side effect).
  5901	# Emitting is a pure append-only ledger write — it never dispatches repair
  5902	# and never triggers a scan (mirror of emit_watchdog_incident_bridge_event).
  5903	# Missing inputs (no workspace, no spec, unreadable spec) FAIL CLOSED: the
  5904	# event cannot be durably recorded without a contract digest, so the caller
  5905	# MUST treat a non-zero return as "do not dispatch" — never succeed
  5906	# eventless.
  5907	emit_runtime_transition_event() {
  5908	  local event_name="$1"
  5909	  local session="$2"
  5910	  local workspace="$3"
  5911	  local remote_spec="$4"
  5912	  local failure_class="${5:-}"
  5913	  local error_text="${6:-}"
  5914	  local candidate_to="${7:-}"
  5915	  local candidate_from="${8:-}"
  5916	  local attempt_num="${9:-}"
  5917	  local summary_text="${10:-}"
  5918	  local chain_digest ledger_rc
  5919	  local -a ledger_args
  5920	  if [[ -z "$workspace" || -z "$remote_spec" || ! -f "$remote_spec" ]]; then
  5921	    printf '[watchdog %s] runtime transition ledger input missing (workspace/spec) for %s; blocking dispatch session=%s\n' \
  5922	      "$(date -Iseconds)" "$event_name" "$session" >>"${LOG:-/dev/null}" 2>&1 || true
  5923	    return 1
  5924	  fi
  5925	  chain_digest="$(PYTHONPATH="$WRAPPER_REPO_ROOT:${SRC_DIR:-$PWD}:${PYTHONPATH:-}" python3 - "$remote_spec" <<'PY'
  5926	import pathlib
  5927	import sys
  5928	
  5929	from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.chain.spec import chain_spec_sha256
  5930	
  5931	print(chain_spec_sha256(pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1])))
  5932	PY
  5933	)"
  5934	  if [[ -z "$chain_digest" ]]; then
  5935	    return 1
  5936	  fi
  5937	  ledger_args=(
  5938	    "$event_name"
  5939	    --root "$workspace"
  5940	    --scope "chain:$session"
  5941	    --session-id "$session"
  5942	    --actor "arnold-watchdog"
  5943	    --failure-class "$failure_class"
  5944	    --error "$error_text"
  5945	    --candidate-to "$candidate_to"
  5946	    --candidate-from "$candidate_from"
  5947	    --attempt "$attempt_num"
  5948	    --chain-spec-sha256 "$chain_digest"
  5949	  )
  5950	  if [[ -n "$summary_text" ]]; then
  5951	    ledger_args+=(--summary "$summary_text")
  5952	  fi
  5953	  PYTHONPATH="$WRAPPER_REPO_ROOT:${SRC_DIR:-$PWD}:${PYTHONPATH:-}" python3 -W ignore::RuntimeWarning \
  5954	    -m arnold_pipelines.megaplan.incident.ledger "${ledger_args[@]}" \
  5955	    >> "${LOG:-/dev/null}" 2>&1
  5956	  ledger_rc=$?
  5957	  if [[ "$ledger_rc" != "0" ]]; then
  5958	    printf '[watchdog %s] runtime transition ledger write failed (%s) rc=%s; blocking dispatch session=%s\n' \
  5959	      "$(date -Iseconds)" "$event_name" "$ledger_rc" "$session" >>"${LOG:-/dev/null}" 2>&1 || true
  5960	    return 1
  5961	  fi
  5962	  return 0
  5963	}
  5964	
  5965	# ---- meta-repair dispatch ----------------------------------------------------
  5966	# Meta-repair diagnoses and fixes repair-system failures when ordinary repair
  5967	# has proven insufficient.  It is gated behind META_REPAIR_ENABLED (default-on),
  5968	# guarded against recursion, and tracked with dedicated marker/pgid sidecars so
  5969	# the watchdog can observe its lifecycle independently of ordinary repair.
  5970	
  5971	run_repair_data_maintenance() {
  5972	  local throttle_secs="${REPAIR_DATA_RETENTION_INTERVAL_SECS:-21600}"
  5973	  [[ "$throttle_secs" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || throttle_secs=21600
  5974	  if (( throttle_secs <= 0 )); then
  5975	    return 0
  5976	  fi
  5977	
  5978	  PYTHONPATH="${WRAPPER_REPO_ROOT}:${SRC_DIR:-$PWD}:${PYTHONPATH:-}" python3 - "$MARKER_DIR" "$REPAIR_DATA_DIR" "$throttle_secs" <<'PY'
  5979	import json
  5980	import pathlib
     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# Ensure the megaplan watchdog is alive in the resident-only container.
     3	#
     4	# Identity-scoped observer, not an env-scoped epic pin. The supervisor
     5	# process MUST NOT inherit ARNOLD_RUNTIME_MANIFEST / ARNOLD_REPAIR_SESSION
     6	# for one epic: those leak into every babysitter dispatch and make the
     7	# watchdog supervise the wrong session with the wrong engine (observed
     8	# 2026-08-26: /tmp pin to megaplan-maintenance on arnold-4a830c6ac9a0, so
     9	# native-build-forward's parked stall was never driven by its own runtime).
    10	# One watchdog scans MARKER_DIR; per-tick identity is the marker session
    11	# -> {manifest_dir}/{session}.json -> epic.runtime_root (T-0022).
    12	#
    13	# This script is the PRODUCER of the in-container launch. There is no
    14	# /tmp consumer. Health is process liveness + a fresh sweep-completed
    15	# receipt, NOT tmux.
    16	set -u
    17	
    18	CONTAINER="${MEGAPLAN_CLOUD_CONTAINER:-megaplan-cloud-agent-resident-only}"
    19	LOG="${MEGAPLAN_WATCHDOG_ENSURE_LOG:-/var/log/megaplan-watchdog-ensure.log}"
    20	WATCHDOG_WRAPPER="${MEGAPLAN_WATCHDOG_WRAPPER:-/workspace/arnold/arnold_pipelines/megaplan/cloud/wrappers/arnold-watchdog}"
    21	WATCHDOG_LOG="${MEGAPLAN_WATCHDOG_LOG:-/tmp/watchdog-ensure.log}"
    22	# Snapshot argv is `bash /tmp/arnold-watchdog.XXXX`. Character-class the
    23	# dot so this is not a literal backslash inside single quotes. Never match
    24	# a bare `arnold-watchdog` substring (this health-check's own pgrep argv).
    25	WATCHDOG_PGREP="pgrep -f 'bash /tmp/arnold-watchdog[.]'"
    26	
    27	now() { date -u +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"; }
    28	log() { printf "[%s] %s\n" "$(now)" "$*" >> "$LOG"; }
    29	
    30	if ! docker inspect "$CONTAINER" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    31	  log "container_missing container=$CONTAINER"
    32	  exit 1
    33	fi
    34	
    35	running="$(docker inspect -f "{{.State.Running}}" "$CONTAINER" 2>/dev/null || echo false)"
    36	if [[ "$running" != "true" ]]; then
    37	  log "container_not_running starting container=$CONTAINER"
    38	  if ! docker start "$CONTAINER" >> "$LOG" 2>&1; then
    39	    log "container_start_failed container=$CONTAINER"
    40	    exit 1
    41	  fi
    42	fi
    43	
    44	STALE_HEARTBEAT_MIN="${MEGAPLAN_WATCHDOG_STALE_HEARTBEAT_MIN:-130}"
    45	SWEEP_COMPLETED="/workspace/.megaplan/status/watchdog-sweep.completed.json"
    46	
    47	watchdog_procs="$(docker exec "$CONTAINER" bash -lc "$WATCHDOG_PGREP | wc -l" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' ')"
    48	if [[ "${watchdog_procs:-0}" -gt 0 ]]; then
    49	  # A sweep that started recently is in-flight, not dead. Restacking every
    50	  # minute kills the babysitter launch (observed 2026-08-26).
    51	  started="$(docker exec "$CONTAINER" bash -lc "if [[ -s /workspace/.megaplan/status/watchdog-sweep.started.json ]] && find /workspace/.megaplan/status/watchdog-sweep.started.json -mmin -10 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then echo recent; else echo old; fi" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' ')"
    52	  stale="$(docker exec "$CONTAINER" bash -lc "if [[ -s '$SWEEP_COMPLETED' ]] && find '$SWEEP_COMPLETED' -mmin +${STALE_HEARTBEAT_MIN} 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then echo stale; else echo fresh; fi" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' ')"
    53	  if [[ "$started" == "recent" || "$stale" != "stale" ]]; then
    54	    log "watchdog_alive container=$CONTAINER procs=${watchdog_procs} started=${started:-?} completed=${stale:-?}"
    55	    exit 0
    56	  fi
    57	  log "watchdog_heartbeat_stale container=$CONTAINER (older than ${STALE_HEARTBEAT_MIN}m); killing to restart"
    58	  docker exec "$CONTAINER" bash -lc "$WATCHDOG_PGREP | xargs -r kill 2>/dev/null || true; sleep 2; true" 2>/dev/null || true
    59	fi
    60	
    61	log "watchdog_missing restarting container=$CONTAINER"
    62	# Observer launch: no per-epic ARNOLD_RUNTIME_MANIFEST / ARNOLD_REPAIR_SESSION.
    63	docker exec -d "$CONTAINER" bash -lc "cd / && unset ARNOLD_RUNTIME_MANIFEST ARNOLD_REPAIR_SESSION ARNOLD_BABYSITTER_SESSION MEGAPLAN_SUPERVISOR_PYTHON PYTHONPATH && export ARNOLD_WATCHDOG_OBSERVER=1 CLOUD_WATCHDOG_INTERVAL_SECS=300 && nohup \"$WATCHDOG_WRAPPER\" >> \"$WATCHDOG_LOG\" 2>&1 & disown; echo launched" >> "$LOG" 2>&1
    64	
    65	sleep 3
    66	watchdog_procs="$(docker exec "$CONTAINER" bash -lc "$WATCHDOG_PGREP | wc -l" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' ')"
    67	if [[ "${watchdog_procs:-0}" -gt 0 ]]; then
    68	  log "watchdog_restarted container=$CONTAINER procs=${watchdog_procs}"
    69	  exit 0
    70	fi
    71	
    72	log "watchdog_restart_failed_not_alive container=$CONTAINER"
    73	exit 1

  7982	        return 0
  7983	    raise SystemExit(f"unsupported resident subagent action: {args.action}")

  7990	def _run_subprocess(argv: list[str], timeout_s: float | None) -> SubagentResult:
  7991	    run_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {"capture_output": True, "text": True, "check": False}
  7992	    if timeout_s is not None:
  7993	        run_kwargs["timeout"] = timeout_s
  7994	    try:
  7995	        completed = subprocess.run(argv, **run_kwargs)
  7996	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
  7997	        return SubagentResult(
  7998	            ok=False,
  7999	            final_text="",
  8000	            stderr=str(exc),
  8001	            returncode=-1,
  8002	            error=(f"subagent timed out after {timeout_s:.0f}s" if timeout_s is not None else "subagent timed out unexpectedly"),
  8003	        )
  8004	    final_text = (completed.stdout or "").strip()
  8005	    stderr = completed.stderr or ""
  8006	    returncode = completed.returncode
  8007	    ok = returncode == 0 and bool(final_text)
  8008	    error: str | None = None
  8009	    if not ok:
  8010	        tail = stderr.strip()[:500]
  8011	        error = f"subagent exit {returncode}" + (f": {tail}" if tail else " (no stdout)")
  8012	    return SubagentResult(
  8013	        ok=ok,
  8014	        final_text=final_text,
  8015	        stderr=stderr,
  8016	        returncode=returncode,
  8017	        error=error,
  8018	    )
  1900	def _is_codex_child(pid, proc):
  1901	    comm = _pid_comm(pid, proc)
  1902	    cmd = _pid_cmdline(pid, proc)
  1903	    return comm == "codex" or comm.startswith("codex") or "codex exec" in cmd

  1906	def _progress_has_tool_line(paths):
  1907	    for path in paths:
  1908	        try:
  1909	            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
  1910	        except OSError:
  1911	            continue
  1912	        if "[tool]" in text or "[done]" in text:
  1913	            return True
  1914	    return False

  1917	def signal_hung_fixer_children(root_pids):
  1918	    """SIGTERM hung ``codex exec`` descendants. Does not touch the babysitter.
  1919	
  1920	    I18 signature: process exists, not R/D, no ESTABLISHED TCP, older than
  1921	    the consult bound. A thinking consult has a socket and is left alone.
  1922	    """
  1923	    proc = _proc_root()
  1924	    if not proc.is_dir():
  1925	        return []
  1926	    bound = float(os.environ.get("CLOUD_WATCHDOG_HUNG_CHILD_MINUTES", "20")) * 60
  1927	    pids = [raw for raw in root_pids if isinstance(raw, int) and raw > 0]
  1928	    signaled = []
  1929	    for pid in _descendant_pids(pids, proc):
  1930	        if not _is_codex_child(pid, proc):
  1931	            continue
  1932	        if _pid_runnable(pid, proc) or _pid_has_established_tcp(pid, proc):
  1933	            continue
  1934	        age = _pid_age_seconds(pid, proc)
  1935	        if age is None or age < bound:
  1936	            continue
  1937	        try:
  1938	            os.kill(pid, 15)
  1939	        except OSError:
  1940	            continue
  1941	        signaled.append(pid)
  1942	    return signaled
  1943	
  1944	
  1945	def babysitter_wedged(payload, run_root):
  1946	    """A live-pid fixer that has made NO progress is wedged.
  1947	
  1948	    Contract (I51b): a busy unbounded babysitter is never reaped. Wedge
  1949	    means: past newborn/first-call grace, no fresh progress file, and the
  1950	    process tree shows no live work. A hung ``codex`` child is not a reason
  1951	    to reap the parent — signal_hung_fixer_children handles that. If /proc
  1952	    cannot be inspected, fail open.
  1953	    """
  1954	    launch_stall_minutes = float(
  1955	        os.environ.get("CLOUD_WATCHDOG_BABYSITTER_LAUNCH_STALL_MINUTES", "5")
  1956	    )
  1957	    log_stall_minutes = float(
  1958	        os.environ.get("CLOUD_WATCHDOG_BABYSITTER_LOG_STALL_MINUTES", "10")
  1959	    )
  1960	    # stdout.log is created at Popen, so log-stall starts at launch and
  1961	    # expires at 10 min while the first DeepSeek call (timeout 600s) may
  1962	    # still have no [tool] line and no ESTABLISHED socket. 20 min covers
  1963	    # API timeout + bootstrap + one scan. Applies ONLY when no [tool]/[done]
  1964	    # has ever been written — not a general wall.
  1965	    first_call_grace_minutes = float(
  1966	        os.environ.get("CLOUD_WATCHDOG_BABYSITTER_FIRST_CALL_GRACE_MINUTES", "20")
  1967	    )
  1968	    try:
  1969	        launched_raw = payload.get("launched_at") or payload.get("dispatched_at")
  1970	        if not launched_raw:
  1971	            return False
  1972	        launched = datetime.fromisoformat(str(launched_raw).replace("Z", "+00:00"))
  1973	        age = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - launched
  1974	        if age < timedelta(minutes=launch_stall_minutes):
  1975	            return False
  2020	        run_root_raw = payload.get("run_root")
  2021	        run_root = pathlib.Path(run_root_raw) if isinstance(run_root_raw, str) else None
  2022	        roots = (payload.get("babysitter_pid"), payload.get("supervisor_pid"))
  2023	        live = pid_live(roots[0]) or pid_live(roots[1])
  2024	        if live:
  2025	            hung = signal_hung_fixer_children(roots)
  2026	            if hung:
  2027	                print(
  2028	                    "hung fixer child; signaling "
  2029	                    f"session={session} pids={hung} run_root={run_root}",
  2030	                    file=sys.stderr,
  2031	                )
  2032	            wedged_now = babysitter_wedged(payload, run_root)
  2033	            # I-2026-08-26-TIMEOUTKILL: a single-scan wedge verdict has repeatedly
  2034	            # SIGTERMed fixers that were mid-call (codex consult, long LLM wait
  2035	            # with momentarily no established socket). Require the wedge to be
  2036	            # observed on TWO scans separated by WEDGE_CONFIRM_SECS (default
  2037	            # 600) before signaling. True hangs still die; mid-call dips live.
  2038	            confirm_secs = float(
  2039	                os.environ.get("CLOUD_WATCHDOG_WEDGE_CONFIRM_SECS", "600")
  2040	            )
  2041	            marker = None
  2042	            if run_root is not None:
  2043	                marker = run_root / "wedged-since.json"
  2044	            if wedged_now:
  2045	                first_seen = None
  2046	                if marker is not None and marker.is_file():
  2047	                    try:
  2048	                        first_seen = float(json.loads(
  2049	                            marker.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  2050	                        ).get("first_seen"))
  2051	                    except Exception:
  2052	                        first_seen = None
  2053	                now_ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
  2054	                if first_seen is None:
  2055	                    if marker is not None:
  2056	                        try:
  2057	                            marker.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  2058	                            marker.write_text(json.dumps({
  2059	                                "first_seen": now_ts,
  2060	                                "session": session,
  2061	                            }, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
  2062	                        except OSError:
  2063	                            pass
  2064	                    raise SystemExit(0)
  2065	                if now_ts - first_seen < confirm_secs:
  2066	                    raise SystemExit(0)
  2067	                if marker is not None:
  2068	                    try:
  2069	                        marker.unlink()
  2070	                    except OSError:
  2071	                        pass
  2072	        # Wedged (or dead): terminate the stale babysitter + its agent tree so
  2073	        # the fresh dispatch below does not race a zombie fixer on the same
  2074	        # chain (observed 2026-08-17 I35: wedged 15:38 agents lingered beside
  2075	        # 16:46 re-dispatches, both alive, competing). Best-effort SIGTERM;
  2076	        # the receipt is left for the fresh launch to supersede.
  2077	        print(
  2078	            "babysitter wedged or dead; signaling "
  2079	            f"session={session} babysitter_pid={payload.get('babysitter_pid')} "
  2080	            f"supervisor_pid={payload.get('supervisor_pid')} "
  2081	            f"run_root={run_root}",
  2082	            file=sys.stderr,
  2083	        )
  2084	        for stale_pid in (payload.get("babysitter_pid"), payload.get("supervisor_pid")):
  2085	            if isinstance(stale_pid, int) and stale_pid > 0 and pid_live(stale_pid):
  2086	                try:
  2087	                    os.kill(stale_pid, 15)
  2088	                except OSError:
  2089	                    pass
  2090	raise SystemExit(1)
  2091	PY
  2092	}
