  2094	# Launch the single-flash babysitter for the given occurrence. Journals
  2095	# deviation_declared + fallback_considered BEFORE the launch side effect (a
  2096	# ledger write failure blocks the launch: no event = no side effect), resolves
  2097	# the goal renderer + arnold-babysitter bin from the LIVE engine, renders the
  2098	# goal, Popens the wrapper detached, grace-polls for an early non-zero rc
  2099	# (fail closed), and writes the dispatch receipt. Returns 0 on launch, 1 on
  2100	# failure. There is NO L1/L2 fallthrough here or at the callers.

   476	def cap_committed_output_text(
   477	    text: str,
   478	    *,
   479	    limit_bytes: int = MAX_COMMITTED_OUTPUT_BYTES,
   480	) -> str:
   481	    """Return *text* capped to *limit_bytes* UTF-8 bytes with a marker."""
   482	    if limit_bytes <= 0:
   483	        raise ValueError("limit_bytes must be positive")
   484	    if not isinstance(text, str):
   485	        raise ValueError("committed output must be a string")
   486	    encoded = text.encode("utf-8")
   487	    if len(encoded) <= limit_bytes:
   488	        return text
   489	    omitted = len(encoded) - limit_bytes
   490	    suffix = _COMMITTED_OUTPUT_TRUNCATION_TEMPLATE.format(omitted=omitted)
   491	    suffix_bytes = suffix.encode("utf-8")
   492	    if len(suffix_bytes) >= limit_bytes:
   493	        return suffix_bytes[:limit_bytes].decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
   494	    allowed = limit_bytes - len(suffix_bytes)
   495	    truncated = encoded[:allowed].decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
   496	    while len((truncated + suffix).encode("utf-8")) > limit_bytes and truncated:
   497	        truncated = truncated[:-1]
   498	    return truncated + suffix

   506	def validate_incident_event(event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   507	    """Validate an incident event and return a normalized shallow copy.
   508	
   509	    Validation rules (M1)
   510	    ----------------------
   511	    * *event* must be a ``dict``.
   512	    * ``schema_version`` must be exactly ``1``.
   513	    * Required string fields: ``event_id``, ``ts``, ``type``, ``actor``,
   514	      ``scope``, ``outcome``, ``summary``.
   515	    * Required nullable string fields: ``next_expected_event``,
   516	      ``deadline_ts``, ``trigger_event_id``.
   517	    * ``ts`` and present ``deadline_ts`` values must be ISO-8601-like
   518	      (``YYYY-MM-DD...``).
   519	    * ``summary`` length must be <= 2048 characters.
   520	    * Required list fields: ``evidence``, ``parent_event_ids``.
   521	
   522	    Forward compatibility
   523	    ---------------------
   524	    Unknown fields present in *event* are preserved in the returned
   525	    shallow copy.
   526	
   527	    Returns
   528	    -------
   529	    dict
   530	        A shallow copy of *event* with all original keys intact.
   531	
   532	    Raises
   533	    ------
   534	    ValueError
   535	        If any validation rule is violated.  The message always names
   536	        the offending field.
   537	    """
   538	    if not isinstance(event, dict):
   539	        raise ValueError("incident event must be a dict")
   540	    # Route only Maintenance event kinds through the strict codec; everything
   541	    # else (including legacy extensions) keeps the permissive M1 path below.
   542	    if is_maintenance_event(event):
   543	        return validate_maintenance_event(event)
   544	    # Reject expanding evidence before recursive regex redaction. This keeps a
   545	    # malformed historical auditor event from consuming gigabytes while the
   546	    # projection layer validates it, and prevents recursive report/decision
   547	    # embedding from entering the append-only ledger in the first place.
   548	    structured_bytes = 0
   549	    for field in ("evidence", "links", "decision", "actions"):
   550	        if field not in event:
   551	            continue
   552	        try:
   553	            encoded = json.dumps(
   554	                event[field],
   555	                sort_keys=True,
   556	                separators=(",", ":"),
   557	                default=str,
   558	            ).encode("utf-8")
   559	        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   560	            raise ValueError(
   561	                f"incident event '{field}' must be JSON serializable"
   562	            ) from exc
   563	        if len(encoded) > MAX_STRUCTURED_FIELD_BYTES:
   564	            raise ValueError(
   565	                f"incident event '{field}' must be <= {MAX_STRUCTURED_FIELD_BYTES} bytes "
   566	                f"(got {len(encoded)})"
   567	            )
   568	        structured_bytes += len(encoded)
   569	    if structured_bytes > MAX_STRUCTURED_FIELD_BYTES:
   570	        raise ValueError(
   571	            "incident event structured fields must be <= "
   572	            f"{MAX_STRUCTURED_FIELD_BYTES} bytes in aggregate (got {structured_bytes})"
   573	        )
   574	    event = redact_incident_event(event)
   575	
   576	    # ── schema_version ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   577	    sv = event.get("schema_version")
   578	    if sv != 1:
   579	        raise ValueError(
   580	            f"incident event schema_version must be 1 (got {sv!r})"
   581	        )
   582	
   583	    # ── required string fields ──────────────────────────────────────
   584	    for field in REQUIRED_M1_FIELDS:
   585	        _check_required_field(event, field)
   586	
   587	    # ── summary length cap ──────────────────────────────────────────
   588	    summary = event["summary"]
   589	    if len(summary) > MAX_SUMMARY_LENGTH:
   590	        raise ValueError(
   591	            f"incident event 'summary' must be <= {MAX_SUMMARY_LENGTH} "
   592	            f"characters (got {len(summary)})"
   593	        )
   594	
   595	    # ── required nullable string fields ─────────────────────────────
   596	    for field in REQUIRED_NULLABLE_STRING_FIELDS:
   597	        _check_string_or_none_field(event, field, required=True)
   598	
   599	    for field in OPTIONAL_NULLABLE_STRING_FIELDS:
   600	        _check_string_or_none_field(event, field, required=False)
   601	
   602	    _check_object_or_none_field(event, "links")
   603	
   604	    # ── timestamps ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   605	    _check_timestamp(event["ts"], "ts")
   606	    deadline_ts = event["deadline_ts"]
   607	    if deadline_ts is not None:
   608	        _check_timestamp(deadline_ts, "deadline_ts")
   609	
   610	    # ── required list fields ────────────────────────────────────────

  1302	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1303	# Single-flash babysitter launch machinery (Q1/Q2). The watchdog's ONLY repair

  1307	# repair-dispatch stack (L1 repair-loop, L2 meta-repair, repair-trigger) has

  5425	watchdog_repair_state_authority_or_reject() {
  5426	  # Steps 59-65: classify a watchdog repair-state mutation (claim write, claim
  5427	  # adoption/existing-owner, stale-state cleanup, process reaping, retry loop,
  5428	  # unchanged-fingerprint escalation, or retry-budget mutation).  A repair-state
  5429	  # mutation may NEVER become accepted repair outside canonical simple_fixer
  5430	  # delegation; authority is never derived from a label, liveness signal, WBC
  5431	  # receipt, or rebuildable projection (SC39).  Forbidden sources emit a typed
  5432	  # zero-authority rejection; exact-F01 occurrences delegate to the simple_fixer
  5433	  # singleton claim with no child agent fan-out.  Emits JSON on stdout with an
  5434	  # ``outcome`` key in {zero_authority_rejected, delegated, no_authority_claim}.
  5435	  local caller_kind="${1:-live_watchdog}"
  5436	  local caller_id="${2:-arnold-watchdog}"
  5437	  local _py_root="${WRAPPER_REPO_ROOT:-${ARNOLD_SRC:-}}"
  5438	  PYTHONSAFEPATH=1 PYTHONPATH="${_py_root:+$_py_root:}${PYTHONPATH:-}" python3 -P - \
  5439	    "$caller_kind" "$caller_id" <<'REPAIR_STATE_PY'
  5440	import json, os, sys
  5441	from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.cloud.wrappers.repair_delegation import (
  5442	    build_repair_delegation, emit_zero_authority_rejection,
  5443	)
  5444	from arnold_pipelines.megaplan.custody.contracts import F01_REPAIR_OCCURRENCE_FIELDS
  5445	
  5446	caller_kind = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "live_watchdog"
  5447	caller_id = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "arnold-watchdog"
  5448	
  5449	forbidden = dict(
  5450	    authority_label=os.environ.get("ARNOLD_REPAIR_AUTHORITY_LABEL", ""),
  5451	    liveness_receipt=os.environ.get("ARNOLD_REPAIR_LIVENESS_RECEIPT", ""),
  5452	    wbc_receipt=os.environ.get("ARNOLD_REPAIR_WBC_RECEIPT", ""),
  5453	    rebuildable_projection=os.environ.get("ARNOLD_REPAIR_REBUILDABLE_PROJECTION", ""),
  5454	)
  5455	if any(v for v in forbidden.values()):
  5456	    result = emit_zero_authority_rejection(
  5457	        caller_kind, caller_id,
  5458	        reason="repair-state mutation authority derived from forbidden source: "
  5459	               + ",".join(sorted(k for k, v in forbidden.items() if v)),
  5460	    )
  5461	    print(json.dumps({
  5462	        "outcome": result.outcome,
  5463	        "delegated": False,
  5464	        "repair_state_mutation": False,
  5465	        "canonical_delegation_path": "simple_fixer.singleton_claim.exact_f01_tuple",
  5466	    }))
  5467	    sys.exit(0)
  5468	
  5469	f01_env = os.environ.get("ARNOLD_REPAIR_F01_OCCURRENCE", "")
  5470	target = {}
  5471	if f01_env:
  5472	    try:
  5473	        target = json.loads(f01_env)
  5474	    except Exception:
  5475	        target = {}
  5476	
  5477	if not target:
  5478	    print(json.dumps({
  5479	        "outcome": "no_authority_claim",
  5480	        "delegated": False,
  5481	        "repair_state_mutation": False,
  5482	        "canonical_delegation_path": "simple_fixer.singleton_claim.exact_f01_tuple",
  5483	    }))
  5484	    sys.exit(0)
  5485	
  5486	for name in F01_REPAIR_OCCURRENCE_FIELDS:
  5487	    target.setdefault(name, "")
  5488	
  5489	delegation = build_repair_delegation(caller_kind, caller_id, target)
  5490	if delegation is None:
  5491	    result = emit_zero_authority_rejection(
  5492	        caller_kind, caller_id,
  5493	        reason="repair-state mutation path could not construct exact F01 occurrence tuple",
  5494	    )
  5495	    print(json.dumps({
  5496	        "outcome": result.outcome,
  5497	        "delegated": False,
  5498	        "repair_state_mutation": False,
  5499	        "canonical_delegation_path": "simple_fixer.singleton_claim.exact_f01_tuple",
  5500	    }))
  5501	    sys.exit(0)
  5502	
  5503	print(json.dumps({
  5504	    "outcome": "delegated",
  5505	    "delegated": True,
  5506	    "repair_state_mutation": True,
  5507	    "canonical_delegation_path": "simple_fixer.singleton_claim.exact_f01_tuple",
  5508	    "occurrence_fingerprint": delegation.occurrence.occurrence_fingerprint,
  5509	}))
  5510	REPAIR_STATE_PY
  5511	}

  8106	    approval_required)
  8107	      log "durable repair goal reached explicit approval gate session=$session goal_id=$repair_goal_id reason=$repair_goal_reason"

  8109	      return 0
  8110	      ;;
  8111	    progressed)
  8112	      log "durable repair goal verified target progress session=$session goal_id=$repair_goal_id reason=$repair_goal_reason"
  8113	      ;;
  8114	  esac

   438	def _populate_incident_placeholders(
   439	    incident: dict[str, Any],
   440	    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   441	) -> None:
   442	    events = sorted(incident["events"], key=lambda item: item["seq"])
   443	    source_fix_seq: int | None = None
   444	    install_sync_applied_seq: int | None = None
   445	    install_sync_applied_has_runtime_identity = False
   446	    install_sync_failed_seq: int | None = None
   447	    repair_retriggered_seq: int | None = None
   448	    full_chain_verified_seq: int | None = None
   449	    repeated_attempt_detected = False
   450	    recovery_without_full_chain = False
   451	    install_activity_without_source_fix = False
   452	    last_attempt_fingerprint: str | None = None
   453	    repeated_attempt_streak = 0
   454	
   455	    for event in events:
   456	        event_type = event["type"]
   457	        seq = event["seq"]
   458	
   459	        if event_type == "repair_attempt":
   460	            attempt_fingerprint = _attempt_fingerprint(event)
   461	            if attempt_fingerprint == last_attempt_fingerprint:
   462	                repeated_attempt_streak += 1
   463	            else:
   464	                last_attempt_fingerprint = attempt_fingerprint
   465	                repeated_attempt_streak = 1
   466	            if repeated_attempt_streak >= 3 and not repeated_attempt_detected:
   467	                repeated_attempt_detected = True
   468	                findings.append(
   469	                    _finding(
   470	                        "loop_break_repeated_attempt_no_new_evidence",
   471	                        message="Repeated repair_attempt events did not add a new hypothesis or state change",
   472	                        incident_id=incident["incident_id"],
   473	                        seq=seq,
   474	                    )
   475	                )
   476	        else:
   477	            last_attempt_fingerprint = None
   478	            repeated_attempt_streak = 0
   479	
   480	        if event_type == "source_fix_committed":
   481	            source_fix_seq = seq
   482	            install_sync_applied_seq = None
   483	            install_sync_applied_has_runtime_identity = False
   484	            install_sync_failed_seq = None
   485	            repair_retriggered_seq = None
   486	            full_chain_verified_seq = None
   487	            continue
   488	
   489	        if event_type == "install_sync_applied":
   490	            if source_fix_seq is None or seq <= source_fix_seq:
   491	                install_activity_without_source_fix = True
   492	                continue
   493	            install_sync_applied_seq = seq
   494	            install_sync_failed_seq = None
   495	            repair_retriggered_seq = None
   660	            if source_fix_seq is not None and seq > source_fix_seq:
   661	                install_sync_applied_seq = seq
   662	                repair_retriggered_seq = None
   663	                status = "mitigated"
   664	        elif event_type == "install_sync_failed":
   665	            source_fix_chain_activity = True
   666	            if source_fix_seq is not None and seq > source_fix_seq:
   667	                install_sync_applied_seq = None
   668	                repair_retriggered_seq = None
   669	                status = "mitigated"
   670	        elif event_type == "repair_retriggered":
   671	            source_fix_chain_activity = True
   672	            if install_sync_applied_seq is not None and seq > install_sync_applied_seq:
   673	                repair_retriggered_seq = seq
   674	                status = "mitigated"
   675	
   676	    problem["recurred_after_fix"] = recurred_after_fix
   677	    problem["status"] = status
   678	
   679	
   680	def _attempt_fingerprint(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   681	    return json.dumps(
   682	        {
   683	            "actor": event["actor"],
   684	            "summary": event["summary"],
   685	            "outcome": event["outcome"],
   686	            "decision": event.get("decision"),
   687	            "actions": event.get("actions"),
   688	        },
   689	        sort_keys=True,
   690	        separators=(",", ":"),
   691	    )
   692	
   693	
   694	def _has_runtime_identity_evidence(evidence: list[Any]) -> bool:
   695	    for item in evidence:
   696	        if not isinstance(item, dict):
   697	            continue
   698	        if item.get("kind") == "runtime_identity":
   699	            return True
   700	    return False

   702	
   703	def _is_terminal_incident_event(event_type: str, outcome: str) -> bool:
   704	    return event_type in _TERMINAL_STATES or outcome == "recovered"
   705	
   200	    (full ISO timestamps, UUIDs) must be applied before less-specific
   201	    ones (bare dates, bare numbers) to prevent partial matches from
   202	    consuming tokens needed for full matches.
   203	    """
   204	    text = str(text or "").strip().lower()
   205	    # Apply most-specific patterns first.
   206	    text = _ISO_TIMESTAMP_RE.sub(" <ts> ", text)
   207	    text = _UUID_RE.sub(" <uuid> ", text)
   208	    text = _HEXISH_TOKEN_RE.sub(" <hex> ", text)
   209	    text = _PID_TOKEN_RE.sub(" <pid> ", text)
   210	    text = _ATTEMPT_TOKEN_RE.sub(" <attempt> ", text)
   211	    text = _ABSOLUTE_PATH_RE.sub(" <path> ", text)
   212	    text = _DURATION_RE.sub(" <duration> ", text)
   213	    # Date-only after ISO timestamps — only bare dates remain.
   214	    text = _DATE_ONLY_RE.sub(" <date> ", text)
   215	    # Bare numbers last — after all structured tokens are replaced.
   216	    text = _BARE_NUMBER_RE.sub(" <n> ", text)
   217	    # Collapse whitespace.
   218	    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()
   219	    # Truncate to a reasonable length floor.
   220	    return text[:1000]
   221	
   222	
   223	def normalize_failure_signature(
   224	    failure_class: FailureClass,
   225	    error: Any,
   226	    *,
   227	    task_id: str = "",
   228	    attempt_id: str = "",
   229	) -> str:
   230	    """Produce a deterministic, content-addressed sha256 signature for a failure.
   231	
   232	    The signature incorporates:
   233	    * ``failure_class`` — the typed failure class
   234	    * ``task_id`` — scoped per-task (empty string means unscoped)
   235	    * ``attempt_id`` — scoped per-attempt (empty string means unscoped)
   236	    * Normalized error attributes: message, error_kind, error_layer,
   237	      exit_kind (if present), and a shallow shape fingerprint.
   238	
   239	    Transient tokens (timestamps, hex IDs, PIDs, absolute paths, etc.)
   240	    are stripped from the message before hashing, so two semantically-
   241	    equivalent failures produce the same signature regardless of when
   242	    or where they occurred.
   243	
   244	    Returns a 64-character lowercase hex digest.
   245	    """
   246	    # Normalize the message.
   247	    raw_message = str(getattr(error, "message", "") or "")
   248	    message = _normalize_message(raw_message)
   249	    raw_command = str(getattr(error, "command", "") or "")
   250	    command = _normalize_message(raw_command)
   251	
   252	    # Extract stable error attributes.
   253	    error_kind = str(getattr(error, "error_kind", "") or "").strip().lower()
   254	    error_layer = str(getattr(error, "error_layer", "") or "").strip().lower()
   255	    exit_kind_raw = getattr(error, "exit_kind", None)
   256	    exit_kind = ""
   257	    if isinstance(exit_kind_raw, ExitKind):
   258	        exit_kind = exit_kind_raw.value
   259	    elif isinstance(exit_kind_raw, str):
   260	        exit_kind = exit_kind_raw.strip().lower()
   261	
   262	    # Provider/status — only include if present (minimal shape).
   263	    provider = str(getattr(error, "provider", "") or "").strip().lower()
   264	    status_code = getattr(error, "status_code", None)
   265	    status_code_str = str(status_code) if status_code is not None else ""
   700	    """
   701	
   702	    max_context_retries: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CONTEXT_RETRIES
   703	    max_external_retries: int = DEFAULT_MAX_EXTERNAL_RETRIES
   704	    max_blocked_retries: int = DEFAULT_MAX_BLOCKED_RETRIES
   705	
   706	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   707	    # The single public surface.
   708	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
   709	
   710	    def classify(
   711	        self,
   712	        error: Any,
   713	        layer: str,
   714	        *,
   715	        context_retries_used: int = 0,
   716	        external_retries_used: int = 0,
   717	        blocked_retries_used: int = 0,
   718	        phase: str = "",
   719	    ) -> RecoveryDecision:
   720	        """Classify ``error`` observed at ``layer`` (phase / orchestration / etc.).
   721	
   722	        Parameters
   723	        ----------
   724	        error
   725	            The error or PhaseResult-like object. May have ``exit_kind``,
   726	            ``error_kind``, ``error_layer``, etc.
   727	        layer
   728	            Where the error surfaced (``"phase"``, ``"orchestration"``, ...).
   729	            Used for diagnostics only; classification is shape-driven.
   730	        context_retries_used / external_retries_used / blocked_retries_used
   731	            Current usage counters (caller-owned). The classifier uses these
   732	            to decide whether the budget is still available.
   733	        phase
   734	            Phase name (e.g. ``"plan"``, ``"execute"``); needed for the
   735	            external-retryable filter.
   736	        """
   737	        exit_kind = self._read_exit_kind(error)
   738	
   739	        # 1) Context exhaustion — caller decides retry_fresh vs halt by budget.
   740	        if exit_kind == ExitKind.context_exhausted or self._mentions_context_exhaustion(error):
   741	            if context_retries_used < self.max_context_retries:
   742	                return RecoveryDecision(
   743	                    action="retry_fresh",
   744	                    budget_delta=1,
   745	                    budget_kind="context",
   746	                    reason="context_exhausted",
   747	                )
   748	            return RecoveryDecision(
   749	                action="halt",
   750	                halt_kind="context_retry_exhausted",
   751	                budget_kind="context",
   752	                reason="context budget exhausted",
   753	            )
   754	
   755	        # 2) Transient external — provider stalls / transport timeouts.
   756	        # Extract nested external_error sub-object if present (PhaseResult carries
   757	        # error attributes one level down); fall back to error itself for bare objects.
   758	        _ext_obj = getattr(error, "external_error", None) or error
   759	        if exit_kind == ExitKind.external_error or self._looks_external(_ext_obj):
   760	            if _is_retryable_external_error(phase, _ext_obj):
   761	                if external_retries_used < self.max_external_retries:
   762	                    return RecoveryDecision(
   763	                        action="retry_transient",
   764	                        budget_delta=1,
   765	                        budget_kind="external",
   766	                        reason="transient external",
   767	                    )
   768	                return RecoveryDecision(
   769	                    action="halt",
   770	                    halt_kind="external_retry_exhausted",
   771	                    budget_kind="external",
   772	                    reason="external budget exhausted",
   773	                )
   774	            # Non-retryable external (auth/billing/etc) — terminal.
   775	            return RecoveryDecision(
   776	                action="halt",
   777	                halt_kind="permanent_external",
   778	                reason="permanent external error",
   779	            )
   780	
   781	        # 3) Blocked-by-prereq / blocked-by-quality — caller may retry.
   782	        if exit_kind in (ExitKind.blocked_by_quality, ExitKind.blocked_by_prereq):
   783	            if blocked_retries_used < self.max_blocked_retries:
   784	                return RecoveryDecision(
   785	                    action="retry_fresh",
   786	                    budget_delta=1,
   787	                    budget_kind="blocked",
   788	                    reason="blocked retry",
   789	                )
   790	            return RecoveryDecision(
   791	                action="halt",
   792	                halt_kind="blocked_retry_exhausted",
   793	                budget_kind="blocked",
   794	                reason="blocked budget exhausted",
   795	            )
   796	
   797	        # 4) Timeout / internal_error — escalate (caller picks next step).
   798	        if exit_kind in (ExitKind.timeout, ExitKind.internal_error):
   799	            return RecoveryDecision(
   800	                action="escalate",
   801	                reason=f"escalate {exit_kind.value if exit_kind else 'unknown'}",
   802	            )
   803	
   804	        # 5) Default — unclassified, halt to be safe.
   805	        return RecoveryDecision(
   806	            action="halt",
   807	            halt_kind="unclassified",
   808	            reason="unclassified error",
   809	        )
   810	
   811	    def classify_with_circuit(
   812	        self,
   813	        error: Any,
   814	        layer: str,
   815	        *,

   475	def classify_model_family(model: str) -> ModelFamily:
   476	    """Classify an already-normalized model name.
   477	
   478	    Raw provider-prefixed names are rejected here. Callers that receive
   479	    provider-prefixed input must normalize through ``resolve_model`` first.
   480	    """
   481	
   482	    name = model.strip()
   483	    lowered = name.lower()
   484	    if not name:
   485	        raise ModelBudgetError(
   486	            "model family classification requires a normalized model name"
   487	        )
   488	    if ":" in name or lowered.startswith(_PROVIDER_PREFIXES):
   489	        raise ModelBudgetError(
   490	            f"raw provider-prefixed model name reached model seam: {model!r}"
   491	        )
   492	    if lowered.startswith(("gpt-", "codex-")) or "codex" in lowered:
   493	        return ModelFamily.CODEX
   494	    if lowered == "claude" or lowered.startswith("claude-"):
   495	        return ModelFamily.CLAUDE
   496	    if lowered.startswith("deepseek-") or lowered.startswith("deepseek/"):
   497	        return ModelFamily.DEEPSEEK
   498	    if lowered.startswith("kimi-") or lowered.startswith("kimi/") or "kimi" in lowered:
   499	        return ModelFamily.KIMI
   500	    if lowered.startswith(("glm-", "glm/", "z-ai/glm")):
   501	        return ModelFamily.GLM
   502	    if "ox-alpha" in lowered or lowered.startswith("stealth/"):
   503	        # OpenRouter stealth/ox-alpha (operator directive 2026-08-24: all
   504	        # native-build-forward phases on ox-alpha). Budget accounting rides
   505	        # the frontier-coding defaults.
   506	        return ModelFamily.CODEX
   507	
   508	    if lowered.startswith(("mimo-", "mimo/")):
   509	        return ModelFamily.MIMO
   510	    raise ModelBudgetError(f"unknown normalized model family: {model!r}")

   512	
   513	def budget_model_input(
   514	    text: str,
   515	    *,
   516	    model: str | None,
   517	    tier: ModelTier,
   518	    max_input_tokens: int | None = None,
   519	) -> ModelBudget:
   520	    """Estimate and enforce the static input budget for an assembled message."""
   521	
   522	    enforced = tier is ModelTier.ENFORCED
   523	    budget_model = _canonical_budget_model_name(model or "")
   524	    try:
   525	        family = classify_model_family(budget_model)
   526	    except ModelBudgetError:
   527	        if enforced:
   528	            raise
   529	        token_count = _fallback_token_count(text)
   530	        limit = max_input_tokens or 32_000

ISSUES:
1. blocker — The structural spy is mis-specified: `_run_step_with_worker` does not exist; use `run_step_with_worker` (`workers/_impl.py:7347`). Avoid the mock early return (`workers/omp.py:1134–1142`), intercept only final spawn/RPC, assert gate-before-spawn, and cover `babysitter/launch.py`—the current criterion tests only two of three doors.
2. major — Naively gating both `_impl` and `run_omp_step` double-gates normal OMP dispatch because `_impl.py:7698–7713` delegates to `run_omp_step`. Gate non-OMP routes in `_impl`, OMP at its backend entry, and babysitter before managed launch; test the nested OMP path gets one total hit.
3. major — Reuse existing admission pieces instead of rebuilding them: OMP already parses/static-validates at `omp.py:1144–1149`, classifies during rendering, and resolves timeout at `:1248`. Add only orchestration and live-membership proof. Crucially, the static catalog still accepts expired `ox-alpha` (`omp.py:98–105`) and family classification accepts it (`arnold/pipeline/model_seam.py:502–506`); the expired-ID test must prove “static accepts, live catalog rejects typed.”
4. blocker — Kill paths still reproduce North-Star anti-patterns. `signal_hung_fixer_children` SIGTERMs on one observation (`arnold-watchdog:1917–1942`) before the two-scan parent-wedge guard (`:2032–2066`). Ensure-restack treats a missing completion receipt as “fresh” and kills after one stale verdict (`ensure-megaplan-watchdog:47–58`). Require durable two-observation confirmation and live-PID plus advancing-sequence health proof at both sites.
5. major — Disposition scope undercounts real terminations. `subprocess.run(timeout=…)` has already killed the launcher child before `TimeoutExpired` (`launch_omp_agent.py:251–261`); resident has two terminate→kill ladders (`subagent.py:4818–4824`, `5065–5072`). Reuse `IncidentLedger.append_event` (`incident/ledger.py:338–361`) through one typed helper and test every actual signal branch.
6. major — Existing fingerprint logic diagnoses rather than prevents: projection flags only the third repeated attempt (`incident/projection.py:452–478`). Add pre-launch enforcement against the last terminal fingerprint, admitting redispatch only with a durable changed-precondition identity.
7. minor — Restore the source brief before fire: `docs/nbf-grok-verdicts.md` is absent; commit `876389b7bd` contains only a truncated 2,175-byte fragment ending in §1, not §3.

2. Use one incident-disposition helper and existing watchdog reap paths; create no parallel ledger.
3. Sequence three batches: fail-first contracts/tests → all wiring → rebase, then rerun the full focused validation once.
